assert(game:GetService("ServerScriptService"):WaitForChild("Services"):WaitForChild("CommunicationsService").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

-- Player text never passes through these remotes. TextChannel owns delivery.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Hierarchy = require(script.Parent.PoliticalHierarchyService)
local Territory = require(script.Parent.TerritoryMapService)
local Diplomacy = require(script.Parent.DiplomacyService)
local AdminConfig = require(script.Parent.AdminConfig)
local Policy = require(script.Parent.CommunicationsPolicy)
local Service = {}

type Context = {
	id: string, kind: string, label: string, owner: string?, instanceId: string?,
	left: number?, right: number?, channel: TextChannel?, adding: { [number]: boolean },
}
local contexts: { [string]: Context } = {}
local started = false
local push: RemoteEvent
local intent: RemoteEvent
local channels: Instance
local queued = false
local excluded: { [number]: boolean } = {}
local lastIntent: { [number]: number } = {}
local snapshotValue: StringValue

local function world(): any
	return Policy.World(Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers, Diplomacy.GetSnapshot().agreements,
		Territory.GetOwnedTerritoryIds, Territory.AreAdjacent)
end

local function observer(player: Player): boolean
	return not excluded[player.UserId] and AdminConfig.IsGameTester(player.UserId)
end

local function eligible(w: any, context: Context, player: Player): boolean
	return not excluded[player.UserId] and Policy.Member(w, context, w.users[player.UserId])
end

local function relationContext(w: any, context: Context): string
	if context.kind == "diplomatic" then
		return "Diplomatic / public political reach"
	end
	if context.kind ~= "direct" then
		return context.label
	end
	local a, b = w.users[context.left], w.users[context.right]
	if a and b then
		local relation = if a.liegeId == b.rulerId or b.liegeId == a.rulerId then "Liege ↔ Subject"
			else "Neighboring rulers"
		return context.label .. " / " .. relation
	end
	return context.label
end

local function canDeliver(context: Context, message: TextChatMessage, target: TextSource): boolean
	local source = message.TextSource
	if not source or not source.CanSend or not context.channel or source.Parent ~= context.channel then
		return false
	end
	local from, to = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId), Players:GetPlayerByUserId(target.UserId)
	if not from or not to or excluded[from.UserId] or excluded[to.UserId] then
		return false
	end
	-- No yielding permission calls or cached membership in this callback.
	local w = world()
	local ordinary = Policy.Deliver(w, context, from.UserId, to.UserId, false)
	local observing = observer(to) and Policy.Deliver(w, context, from.UserId, to.UserId, true)
	if not ordinary and not observing then
		return false
	end
	-- Labels alone cannot display text: client joins this envelope to native Success receipt.
	push:FireClient(to, "Identity", {
		messageId = message.MessageId, contextId = context.id, kind = context.kind,
		label = context.label, ordinary = ordinary, observing = observing,
		prefix = Policy.Prefix(w, context, from.UserId, to.UserId, false),
		observerPrefix = "[" .. relationContext(w, context) .. "] " .. Policy.Prefix(w, context, from.UserId, to.UserId, true),
	})
	return true
end

local function create(id: string, kind: string, label: string, fields: any?): Context
	local existing = contexts[id]
	if existing then
		return existing
	end
	local context: Context = {
		id = id, kind = kind, label = label, adding = {},
		owner = fields and fields.owner, instanceId = fields and fields.instanceId,
		left = fields and fields.left, right = fields and fields.right,
	}
	local channel = Instance.new("TextChannel")
	channel.Name = "Comms_" .. id
	channel:SetAttribute("CommunicationsContextId", id)
	channel.Parent = channels
	context.channel = channel
	contexts[id] = context
	channel.ShouldDeliverCallback = function(message, target)
		return canDeliver(context, message, target)
	end
	return context
end

local function stateFor(player: Player, w: any): any
	local rows, contacts = {}, {}
	for _, context in contexts do
		local member = eligible(w, context, player)
		if member or observer(player) then
			table.insert(rows, { id = context.id, kind = context.kind, label = context.label,
				channelName = context.channel and context.channel.Name or "", canSend = member, observerOnly = not member })
		end
	end
	local me = w.users[player.UserId]
	for _, other in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if other ~= player and not excluded[other.UserId] and Policy.Direct(w, me, w.users[other.UserId]) then
			table.insert(contacts, { userId = other.UserId, name = other.Name })
		end
	end
	table.sort(rows, function(a, b) return a.id < b.id end)
	table.sort(contacts, function(a, b) return a.name < b.name end)
	return { contexts = rows, contacts = contacts, isAdmin = observer(player) }
end

local function sync()
	local w = world()
	create("diplomatic", "diplomatic", "Diplomatic")
	for _, ruler in w.rulers do
		if #ruler.vassalIds > 0 then
			create("council:" .. ruler.rulerId, "council", "Council — " .. ruler.displayName, { owner = ruler.rulerId })
		end
	end
	for _, agreement in w.agreements do
		if agreement.alliance and agreement.allianceInstanceId ~= "" then
			local a, b = w.rulers[agreement.leftRulerId], w.rulers[agreement.rightRulerId]
			if a and b then
				create("alliance:" .. agreement.allianceInstanceId, "alliance", "Allies — " .. a.displayName .. " ↔ " .. b.displayName,
					{ instanceId = agreement.allianceInstanceId })
			end
		end
	end
	for _, context in contexts do
		local channel = context.channel
		if not channel then continue end
		local wanted: { [number]: boolean } = {}
		for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
			if eligible(w, context, player) or observer(player) then wanted[player.UserId] = true end
		end
		local present: { [number]: boolean } = {}
		for _, source in channel:GetChildren() do
			if source:IsA("TextSource") then
				local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId)
				if not wanted[source.UserId] or not player then
					source.CanSend = false
					source:Destroy() -- History is local; retained recipients are not history.
				else
					source.CanSend = eligible(w, context, player)
					present[source.UserId] = true
				end
			end
		end
		for userId in wanted do
			if present[userId] or context.adding[userId] then continue end
			context.adding[userId] = true
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, source = pcall(function() return channel:AddUserAsync(userId) end)
				context.adding[userId] = nil
				if ok and source and source:IsA("TextSource") then
					local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
					local fresh = world()
					if not player or not (eligible(fresh, context, player) or observer(player)) then
						source:Destroy()
					else
						source.CanSend = eligible(fresh, context, player)
					end
				elseif not ok then
					warn("[Communications] native membership denied:", userId, tostring(source))
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		if not excluded[player.UserId] then push:FireClient(player, "State", stateFor(player, w)) end
	end
	if snapshotValue then snapshotValue.Value = HttpService:JSONEncode(Service.DebugInspect()) end
end

local function queueSync()
	if queued then return end
	queued = true
	task.defer(function() queued = false; sync() end)
end

function Service.OpenDirect(player: Player, targetId: any): (boolean, string)
	if typeof(targetId) ~= "number" or targetId % 1 ~= 0 or targetId == player.UserId then
		return false, "Invalid contact."
	end
	local target = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(targetId)
	local w = world()
	if not target or excluded[player.UserId] or excluded[targetId] or not Policy.Direct(w, w.users[player.UserId], w.users[targetId]) then
		return false, "Direct requires a current border."
	end
	local left, right = math.min(player.UserId, targetId), math.max(player.UserId, targetId)
	local id = "direct:" .. left .. ":" .. right
	local context = contexts[id]
	if not context then
		context = create(id, "direct", "Direct — " .. player.Name .. " ↔ " .. target.Name, { left = left, right = right })
		local ok, err = pcall(function() (context.channel :: TextChannel):SetDirectChatRequester(player) end)
		if not ok then
			(context.channel :: TextChannel):Destroy()
			contexts[id] = nil
			return false, "Native Direct unavailable: " .. tostring(err)
		end
	end
	sync()
	push:FireClient(player, "Opened", id)
	return true, id
end

function Service.DebugInspect(): any
	local w = world()
	local result: any = { implementation = "NativeDeliveryV01", contexts = {} }
	for _, context in contexts do
		local members, sources = {}, {}
		for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
			if eligible(w, context, player) then table.insert(members, player.UserId) end
		end
		if context.channel then
			for _, source in context.channel:GetChildren() do
				if source:IsA("TextSource") then table.insert(sources, { userId = source.UserId, canSend = source.CanSend }) end
			end
		end
		table.insert(result.contexts, { id = context.id, kind = context.kind, members = members, sources = sources })
	end
	return result
end

function Service.DebugCanDeliver(id: string, from: number, to: number, asObserver: boolean?): boolean
	local context = contexts[id]
	if not context or excluded[from] or excluded[to] then return false end
	return Policy.Deliver(world(), context, from, to, asObserver == true)
end

function Service.Start()
	if started then return end
	started = true
	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	intent = remotes:WaitForChild("CommunicationsClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	push = remotes:WaitForChild("CommunicationsServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels") :: Instance
	if not channels then
		channels = Instance.new("Folder")
		channels.Name = "TextChannels"
		channels.Parent = TextChatService
	end
	-- No unrestricted parallel default room, team room, or whisper path.
	for _, child in channels:GetChildren() do
		if child:IsA("TextChannel") then child.ShouldDeliverCallback = function() return false end end
	end
	channels.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child:IsA("TextChannel") and not child:GetAttribute("CommunicationsContextId") then
			child.ShouldDeliverCallback = function() return false end
		end
	end)
	Hierarchy.StateChanged:Connect(queueSync)
	Territory.OwnershipChanged:Connect(queueSync)
	Diplomacy.StateChanged:Connect(queueSync)
	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player) excluded[player.UserId] = nil; queueSync() end)
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player) excluded[player.UserId] = true; lastIntent[player.UserId] = nil; queueSync() end)
	intent.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, action, arg)
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - (lastIntent[player.UserId] or -math.huge) < 0.15 then return end
		lastIntent[player.UserId] = now
		if action == "Ready" then
			push:FireClient(player, "State", stateFor(player, world()))
		elseif action == "OpenDirect" then
			local ok, message = Service.OpenDirect(player, arg)
			if not ok then push:FireClient(player, "Notice", message) end
		else
			push:FireClient(player, "Notice", "Unsupported Communications intent.")
		end
	end)
	local debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "CommunicationsDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = script.Parent.Parent
	snapshotValue = Instance.new("StringValue")
	snapshotValue.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	snapshotValue.Parent = debugFolder
	local invoke = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
	invoke.Name = "Invoke"
	invoke.Parent = debugFolder
	invoke.OnInvoke = function(action, a, b, c)
		if action == "Inspect" then return Service.DebugInspect() end
		if action == "Sync" then sync(); return true end
		if action == "CanDeliver" then return Service.DebugCanDeliver(a, b, c) end
		if action == "OpenDirect" then
			local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(a)
			if player then return Service.OpenDirect(player, b) end
		end
		return false, "Unknown debug operation."
	end
	sync()
	print("[Communications] NativeDeliveryV01 started.")
end

return Service

assert(game:GetService("ServerScriptService"):WaitForChild("Services"):WaitForChild("DiplomacyService").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

--[[
	Diplomacy V0.1.

	Server-authoritative Alliance and NAP between independent root rulers.
	Consumes Political Hierarchy; does not duplicate the tree.
	No warfare, map, or title effects.
]]

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.DiplomacyConfig)
local Hierarchy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.PoliticalHierarchyService)

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local DiplomacyService = {}

local stateChangedBindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
DiplomacyService.StateChanged = stateChangedBindable.Event

type Agreement = {
	leftId: string,
	rightId: string,
	alliance: boolean,
	allianceInstanceId: string?,
	nap: boolean,
}

type DipRequest = {
	requestId: string,
	agreementType: string,
	fromRulerId: string,
	toRulerId: string,
	createdAt: number,
	expiresAt: number,
	expireThread: thread?,
}

local started = false
local agreementsByKey: { [string]: Agreement } = {}
local requestsById: { [string]: DipRequest } = {}
local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?

local function now(): number
	return os.clock()
end

local function pairKey(a: string, b: string): string
	if a < b then
		return a .. "|" .. b
	end
	return b .. "|" .. a
end

local function orderedPair(a: string, b: string): (string, string)
	if a < b then
		return a, b
	end
	return b, a
end

local function lifetime(): number
	local value = Config.RequestLifetimeSeconds
	if typeof(value) ~= "number" or value <= 0 then
		return 30
	end
	return value
end

local function findRuler(rulerId: string): any?
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.rulerId == rulerId then
			return ruler
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function isIndependentRoot(ruler: any?): boolean
	return ruler ~= nil and ruler.independent == true
end

local function noticePlayer(player: Player, message: string)
	print("[Diplomacy] notice ->", player.Name, message)
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "Notice", message)
end

local function noticeRulerId(rulerId: string, message: string)
	local ruler = findRuler(rulerId)
	local userId = ruler and tonumber(ruler.userId)
	if typeof(userId) == "number" and userId ~= 0 then
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		if player then
			noticePlayer(player, message)
		end
	end
end

local function cancelExpire(request: DipRequest)
	local thread = request.expireThread
	request.expireThread = nil
	if thread then
		pcall(function()
			task.cancel(thread)
		end)
	end
end

local function removeRequest(requestId: string)
	local request = requestsById[requestId]
	if not request then
		return
	end
	cancelExpire(request)
	requestsById[requestId] = nil
end

local function getOrCreateAgreement(a: string, b: string): Agreement
	local key = pairKey(a, b)
	local existing = agreementsByKey[key]
	if existing then
		return existing
	end
	local left, right = orderedPair(a, b)
	local created: Agreement = {
		leftId = left,
		rightId = right,
		alliance = false,
		nap = false,
	}
	agreementsByKey[key] = created
	return created
end

local function pruneEmpty(agreement: Agreement)
	if not agreement.alliance and not agreement.nap then
		agreementsByKey[pairKey(agreement.leftId, agreement.rightId)] = nil
	end
end

local function findPendingSameType(a: string, b: string, agreementType: string): DipRequest?
	local t = now()
	for _, request in requestsById do
		if request.agreementType == agreementType and request.expiresAt > t then
			local same = pairKey(request.fromRulerId, request.toRulerId) == pairKey(a, b)
			if same then
				return request
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function buildSnapshot()
	local agreements = {}
	for _, agreement in agreementsByKey do
		if agreement.alliance or agreement.nap then
			table.insert(agreements, {
				leftRulerId = agreement.leftId,
				rightRulerId = agreement.rightId,
				alliance = agreement.alliance,
				allianceInstanceId = agreement.allianceInstanceId or "",
				nap = agreement.nap,
			})
		end
	end
	table.sort(agreements, function(x, y)
		if x.leftRulerId ~= y.leftRulerId then
			return x.leftRulerId < y.leftRulerId
		end
		return x.rightRulerId < y.rightRulerId
	end)
	local requests = {}
	local t = now()
	for _, request in requestsById do
		if request.expiresAt > t then
			table.insert(requests, {
				requestId = request.requestId,
				agreementType = request.agreementType,
				fromRulerId = request.fromRulerId,
				toRulerId = request.toRulerId,
				expiresAt = request.expiresAt,
			})
		end
	end
	return {
		agreements = agreements,
		requests = requests,
	}
end

local function pushState(toPlayer: Player?)
	if not toPlayer then
		stateChangedBindable:Fire()
	end
	local payload = buildSnapshot()
	local encoded = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
	if debugSnapshot then
		debugSnapshot.Value = encoded
	end
	if toPlayer then
		print("[Diplomacy] push State to", toPlayer.Name)
		serverPush:FireClient(toPlayer, "State", encoded)
	else
		print("[Diplomacy] push State all clients agreements=", #payload.agreements, "requests=", #payload.requests)
		serverPush:FireAllClients("State", encoded)
	end
end

local function typeLabel(agreementType: string): string
	if agreementType == "NAP" then
		return "Non-Aggression Pact"
	end
	return "Alliance"
end

function DiplomacyService.GetSnapshot()
	return buildSnapshot()
end

function DiplomacyService.AreAllied(rulerA: string, rulerB: string): boolean
	local a = findRuler(rulerA)
	local b = findRuler(rulerB)
	if not a or not b then
		return false
	end
	local ra = a.rootRulerId
	local rb = b.rootRulerId
	if ra == rb then
		return false
	end
	local agreement = agreementsByKey[pairKey(ra, rb)]
	return agreement ~= nil and agreement.alliance == true
end

function DiplomacyService.HasNonAggressionPact(rulerA: string, rulerB: string): boolean
	local a = findRuler(rulerA)
	local b = findRuler(rulerB)
	if not a or not b then
		return false
	end
	local ra = a.rootRulerId
	local rb = b.rootRulerId
	if ra == rb then
		return false
	end
	local agreement = agreementsByKey[pairKey(ra, rb)]
	return agreement ~= nil and agreement.nap == true
end

local function dissolveInvolving(rulerId: string, reason: string)
	local removedAny = false
	for key, agreement in agreementsByKey do
		if agreement.leftId == rulerId or agreement.rightId == rulerId then
			local other = if agreement.leftId == rulerId then agreement.rightId else agreement.leftId
			if agreement.alliance then
				noticeRulerId(other, "Alliance dissolved (" .. reason .. ").")
			end
			if agreement.nap then
				noticeRulerId(other, "Non-Aggression Pact dissolved (" .. reason .. ").")
			end
			agreementsByKey[key] = nil
			removedAny = true
		end
	end
	local t = now()
	for requestId, request in requestsById do
		if request.fromRulerId == rulerId or request.toRulerId == rulerId then
			if request.expiresAt > t then
				local other = if request.fromRulerId == rulerId then request.toRulerId else request.fromRulerId
				noticeRulerId(other, typeLabel(request.agreementType) .. " offer is no longer valid.")
			end
			removeRequest(requestId)
			removedAny = true
		end
	end
	if removedAny then
		noticeRulerId(rulerId, "External diplomatic agreements ended (" .. reason .. ").")
		pushState()
	end
end

function DiplomacyService.Offer(fromRulerId: string, toRulerId: string, agreementType: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if agreementType ~= "Alliance" and agreementType ~= "NAP" then
		return false, "Unknown agreement type."
	end
	if fromRulerId == toRulerId then
		return false, "Cannot conduct diplomacy with yourself."
	end
	local fromRuler = findRuler(fromRulerId)
	local toRuler = findRuler(toRulerId)
	if not isIndependentRoot(fromRuler) or not isIndependentRoot(toRuler) then
		return false, "Only independent rulers may conduct this diplomacy."
	end
	local agreement = agreementsByKey[pairKey(fromRulerId, toRulerId)]
	if agreementType == "Alliance" and agreement and agreement.alliance then
		return false, "Alliance already exists."
	end
	if agreementType == "NAP" and agreement and agreement.nap then
		return false, "Non-Aggression Pact already exists."
	end
	if findPendingSameType(fromRulerId, toRulerId, agreementType) then
		return false, "A matching offer is already pending."
	end
	local requestId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	local created = now()
	local ttl = lifetime()
	local request: DipRequest = {
		requestId = requestId,
		agreementType = agreementType,
		fromRulerId = fromRulerId,
		toRulerId = toRulerId,
		createdAt = created,
		expiresAt = created + ttl,
		expireThread = nil,
	}
	request.expireThread = task.delay(ttl, function()
		local expired = requestsById[requestId]
		if expired then
			expired.expireThread = nil
			removeRequest(requestId)
			pushState()
			noticeRulerId(expired.fromRulerId, typeLabel(expired.agreementType) .. " offer expired.")
			noticeRulerId(expired.toRulerId, typeLabel(expired.agreementType) .. " offer expired.")
		end
	end)
	requestsById[requestId] = request
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(toRulerId, typeLabel(agreementType) .. " offered.")
	return true, requestId
end

function DiplomacyService.Decline(requestId: string, actorRulerId: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local request = requestsById[requestId]
	if not request then
		return false, "Request is no longer valid."
	end
	if now() >= request.expiresAt then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, "Request has expired."
	end
	if actorRulerId ~= request.toRulerId then
		return false, "Only the recipient may decline this offer."
	end
	local fromId = request.fromRulerId
	local toId = request.toRulerId
	local label = typeLabel(request.agreementType)
	removeRequest(requestId)
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(toId, label .. " declined.")
	noticeRulerId(fromId, label .. " declined.")
	return true, label .. " declined."
end

function DiplomacyService.Accept(requestId: string, actorRulerId: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local request = requestsById[requestId]
	if not request then
		return false, "Request is no longer valid."
	end
	if now() >= request.expiresAt then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, "Request has expired."
	end
	if actorRulerId ~= request.toRulerId then
		return false, "Only the recipient may accept this offer."
	end
	local fromRuler = findRuler(request.fromRulerId)
	local toRuler = findRuler(request.toRulerId)
	if not isIndependentRoot(fromRuler) or not isIndependentRoot(toRuler) then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, "Only independent rulers may conduct this diplomacy."
	end
	local agreement = getOrCreateAgreement(request.fromRulerId, request.toRulerId)
	if request.agreementType == "Alliance" then
		if agreement.alliance then
			removeRequest(requestId)
			pushState()
			return false, "Alliance already exists."
		end
		agreement.alliance = true
		agreement.allianceInstanceId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	else
		if agreement.nap then
			removeRequest(requestId)
			pushState()
			return false, "Non-Aggression Pact already exists."
		end
		agreement.nap = true
	end
	local label = typeLabel(request.agreementType)
	removeRequest(requestId)
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(request.toRulerId, label .. " accepted.")
	noticeRulerId(request.fromRulerId, label .. " accepted.")
	return true, label .. " accepted."
end

function DiplomacyService.Break(fromRulerId: string, toRulerId: string, agreementType: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if agreementType ~= "Alliance" and agreementType ~= "NAP" then
		return false, "Unknown agreement type."
	end
	local fromRuler = findRuler(fromRulerId)
	if not isIndependentRoot(fromRuler) then
		return false, "Only independent rulers may break this agreement."
	end
	local agreement = agreementsByKey[pairKey(fromRulerId, toRulerId)]
	if not agreement then
		return false, "No such agreement."
	end
	if agreementType == "Alliance" then
		if not agreement.alliance then
			return false, "No Alliance with that realm."
		end
		agreement.alliance = false
		agreement.allianceInstanceId = nil
	else
		if not agreement.nap then
			return false, "No Non-Aggression Pact with that realm."
		end
		agreement.nap = false
	end
	pruneEmpty(agreement)
	pushState()
	local label = typeLabel(agreementType)
	noticeRulerId(fromRulerId, label .. " broken.")
	noticeRulerId(toRulerId, label .. " broken.")
	return true, label .. " broken."
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any, a: any, b: any)
	if typeof(action) ~= "string" then
		noticePlayer(player, "Invalid request.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Ready" then
		print("[Diplomacy] Ready from", player.Name)
		pushState(player)
		return
	end
	local fromId = Hierarchy.GetRulerIdForUserId(player.UserId)
	if not fromId then
		noticePlayer(player, "No ruler record.")
		return
	end
	if action == "OfferAlliance" or action == "OfferNAP" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			noticePlayer(player, "Invalid target.")
			return
		end
		local kind = if action == "OfferAlliance" then "Alliance" else "NAP"
		local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Offer(fromId, a, kind)
		if not ok then
			noticePlayer(player, result)
		else
			noticePlayer(player, typeLabel(kind) .. " offered.")
		end
		return
	end
	if action == "Accept" or action == "Decline" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			noticePlayer(player, "Invalid request.")
			return
		end
		local ok, result
		if action == "Accept" then
			ok, result = DiplomacyService.Accept(a, fromId)
		else
			ok, result = DiplomacyService.Decline(a, fromId)
		end
		if not ok then
			noticePlayer(player, result)
		end
		return
	end
	if action == "BreakAlliance" or action == "BreakNAP" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			noticePlayer(player, "Invalid target.")
			return
		end
		local kind = if action == "BreakAlliance" then "Alliance" else "NAP"
		local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Break(fromId, a, kind)
		if not ok then
			noticePlayer(player, result)
		end
		return
	end
	noticePlayer(player, "Unknown action.")
end

local function syncIndependence()
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.independent ~= true then
			dissolveInvolving(ruler.rulerId, "no longer independent")
		end
	end
end

function DiplomacyService.Start()
	if started then
		return
	end
	started = true

	local remotesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes") :: Folder
	clientIntent = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("DiplomacyClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	serverPush = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("DiplomacyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	clientIntent.OnServerEvent:Connect(onClientIntent)

	local debugFolder = ServerScriptService:FindFirstChild("DiplomacyDebug")
	if debugFolder then
		debugFolder:Destroy()
	end
	debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "DiplomacyDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = ServerScriptService
	debugSnapshot = Instance.new("StringValue")
	debugSnapshot.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	debugSnapshot.Parent = debugFolder
	local help = Instance.new("StringValue")
	help.Name = "Help"
	help.Value =
		"Prototype only. Invoke Offer/Accept/Decline/Break; or DipOffer=fromId=toId=Alliance|NAP; Accept=requestId=actorId; Break=fromId=toId=Alliance|NAP"
	help.Parent = debugFolder
	local invoke = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
	invoke.Name = "Invoke"
	invoke.Parent = debugFolder
	invoke.OnInvoke = function(action: any, a: any, b: any, c: any)
		if action == "Offer" then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Offer(a, b, c)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Invoke Offer", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Accept" then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Accept(a, b)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Invoke Accept", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Decline" then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Decline(a, b)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Invoke Decline", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Break" then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Break(a, b, c)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Invoke Break", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		end
		return false, "Unknown debug action."
	end

	debugFolder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("DipOffer"):Connect(function()
		local raw = debugFolder:GetAttribute("DipOffer")
		if typeof(raw) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local fromId, toId, kind = string.match(raw, "^(.-)=(.-)=(Alliance|NAP)$")
		if fromId and toId and kind then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Offer(fromId, toId, kind)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Offer", ok, result)
		end
	end)
	debugFolder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Accept"):Connect(function()
		local raw = debugFolder:GetAttribute("Accept")
		if typeof(raw) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local requestId, actorId = string.match(raw, "^(.-)=(.+)$")
		if requestId and actorId then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Accept(requestId, actorId)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Accept", ok, result)
		end
	end)
	debugFolder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Break"):Connect(function()
		local raw = debugFolder:GetAttribute("Break")
		if typeof(raw) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local fromId, toId, kind = string.match(raw, "^(.-)=(.-)=(Alliance|NAP)$")
		if fromId and toId and kind then
			local ok, result = DiplomacyService.Break(fromId, toId, kind)
			print("[Diplomacy] debug Break", ok, result)
		end
	end)

	_G.__DiplomacyV01 = DiplomacyService

	Hierarchy.StateChanged:Connect(syncIndependence)
	Hierarchy.RulerRemoving:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		dissolveInvolving(rulerId, "left the match")
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		task.defer(function()
			if player.Parent then
				pushState(player)
			end
		end)
	end)

	pushState()
	print("[Diplomacy] service started")
end

return DiplomacyService
]==========],"ServerScriptService/Services/DiplomacyService.luau");

--[[
	Economy / Resources V0.1 — server-authoritative Gold.

	Reads Hierarchy ruler lifecycle and Territory ownership/config.
	Does not own those systems. Does not grant titles, Prestige, wars, or tax.
]]

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local EconomyConfig = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.EconomyConfig)
local EconomyPayout = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.EconomyPayout)
local Hierarchy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.PoliticalHierarchyService)
local Territory = require(ServerScriptService.Services.TerritoryMapService)

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local EconomyService = {}

local started = false
local goldByRuler: { [string]: number } = {}
local nextPayoutAt = 0
local payoutLoopRunning = false

local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?

local function findRuler(rulerId: string): any?
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.rulerId == rulerId then
			return ruler
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function definitionById()
	local byId = {}
	for _, t in Territory.GetMapDefinition().territories do
		byId[t.id] = t
	end
	return byId
end

local function isWholeNonNegative(value: number): boolean
	return typeof(value) == "number" and value >= 0 and value == math.floor(value) and value == value
end

local function ensureGold(rulerId: string)
	if goldByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		goldByRuler[rulerId] = EconomyConfig.StartingGold
	end
end

function EconomyService.GetBalance(rulerId: string): number
	local value = goldByRuler[rulerId]
	if typeof(value) == "number" then
		return value
	end
	return 0
end

function EconomyService.GetIncomeGoldPerMinute(rulerId: string): number
	if goldByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return 0
	end
	local byId = definitionById()
	local total = 0
	for _, territoryId in Territory.GetOwnedTerritoryIds(rulerId) do
		local def = byId[territoryId]
		if def and typeof(def.baseGoldIncome) == "number" then
			total += def.baseGoldIncome
		end
	end
	return total
end

local function playerSnapshot(rulerId: string)
	return {
		gold = EconomyService.GetBalance(rulerId),
		incomeGoldPerMinute = EconomyService.GetIncomeGoldPerMinute(rulerId),
		payoutIntervalSeconds = EconomyConfig.PayoutIntervalSeconds,
		nextPayoutAt = nextPayoutAt,
	}
end

local function inspectSnapshot(rulerId: string)
	return {
		rulerId = rulerId,
		gold = EconomyService.GetBalance(rulerId),
		incomeGoldPerMinute = EconomyService.GetIncomeGoldPerMinute(rulerId),
		ownedTerritoryCount = #Territory.GetOwnedTerritoryIds(rulerId),
	}
end

local function pushPlayer(player: Player)
	if not started then
		return
	end
	local rulerId = Hierarchy.GetRulerIdForUserId(player.UserId)
	local payload
	if rulerId and goldByRuler[rulerId] ~= nil then
		payload = playerSnapshot(rulerId)
	else
		payload = {
			gold = 0,
			incomeGoldPerMinute = 0,
			payoutIntervalSeconds = EconomyConfig.PayoutIntervalSeconds,
			nextPayoutAt = nextPayoutAt,
		}
	end
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "State", HttpService:JSONEncode(payload))
end

local function pushAllPlayers()
	if debugSnapshot then
		local rows = {}
		for rulerId, gold in goldByRuler do
			table.insert(rows, {
				rulerId = rulerId,
				gold = gold,
				incomeGoldPerMinute = EconomyService.GetIncomeGoldPerMinute(rulerId),
			})
		end
		table.sort(rows, function(a, b)
			return a.rulerId < b.rulerId
		end)
		debugSnapshot.Value = HttpService:JSONEncode({
			nextPayoutAt = nextPayoutAt,
			rulers = rows,
		})
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		pushPlayer(player)
	end
end

local function notice(player: Player, message: string)
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "Notice", message)
end

function EconomyService.GetInspect(rulerId: string): any?
	if goldByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return nil
	end
	return inspectSnapshot(rulerId)
end

function EconomyService.AddGold(rulerId: string, amount: number): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if goldByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No economy state."
	end
	if not isWholeNonNegative(amount) then
		return false, "Gold amount must be a whole number >= 0."
	end
	if amount == 0 then
		return true, "No change."
	end
	goldByRuler[rulerId] += amount
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Added %d Gold.", amount)
end

function EconomyService.TrySpend(rulerId: string, amount: number): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if goldByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No economy state."
	end
	if typeof(amount) ~= "number" or amount ~= amount or amount ~= math.floor(amount) or amount <= 0 then
		return false, "Spend amount must be a whole number greater than 0."
	end
	local balance = goldByRuler[rulerId]
	if amount > balance then
		return false, "Insufficient Gold."
	end
	goldByRuler[rulerId] = balance - amount
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Spent %d Gold.", amount)
end

function EconomyService.DebugSetGold(rulerId: string, value: number): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if goldByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No economy state."
	end
	if not isWholeNonNegative(value) then
		return false, "Gold must be a whole number >= 0."
	end
	goldByRuler[rulerId] = value
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Gold set to %d.", value)
end

local function payoutTick()
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return end
	local byId = definitionById()
	local credit: { [string]: number } = {}
	for _, t in Territory.GetMapDefinition().territories do
		local ownerId = Territory.GetOwnerRulerId(t.id)
		if ownerId and goldByRuler[ownerId] ~= nil then
			local def = byId[t.id]
			local perTick = if def then EconomyPayout.GoldPerTick(def.baseGoldIncome) else nil
			if perTick and perTick > 0 then
				credit[ownerId] = (credit[ownerId] or 0) + perTick
			end
		end
	end
	for rulerId, amount in credit do
		goldByRuler[rulerId] += amount
	end
end

local function runPayoutLoop()
	if payoutLoopRunning then
		return
	end
	payoutLoopRunning = true
	local interval = EconomyConfig.PayoutIntervalSeconds
	nextPayoutAt = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + interval
	task.spawn(function()
		pushAllPlayers()
		while started do
			local waitFor = nextPayoutAt - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
			if waitFor > 0 then
				task.wait(waitFor)
			end
			if not started then
				break
			end
			payoutTick()
			nextPayoutAt = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + interval
			pushAllPlayers()
		end
		payoutLoopRunning = false
	end)
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any)
	if typeof(action) ~= "string" then
		return
	end
	if action == "Ready" then
		pushPlayer(player)
		return
	end
	notice(player, "Unknown action.")
end

function EconomyService.Start()
	if started then
		return
	end

	local mapOk, mapErr = EconomyPayout.ValidateMap(Territory.GetMapDefinition())
	if not mapOk then
		error("[Economy] " .. (mapErr or "Invalid map Gold configuration."))
	end

	started = true

	local remotesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes") :: Folder
	clientIntent = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("EconomyClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	serverPush = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("EconomyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	clientIntent.OnServerEvent:Connect(onClientIntent)

	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		ensureGold(ruler.rulerId)
	end

	local debugFolder = ServerScriptService:FindFirstChild("EconomyDebug")
	if debugFolder then
		debugFolder:Destroy()
	end
	debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "EconomyDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = ServerScriptService
	local help = Instance.new("StringValue")
	help.Name = "Help"
	help.Value =
		"Prototype only. Invoke('Inspect'|'SetGold'|'AddGold'|'SpendGold', rulerId, amount). Prefer AdminBridge/Kohl."
	help.Parent = debugFolder
	debugSnapshot = Instance.new("StringValue")
	debugSnapshot.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	debugSnapshot.Parent = debugFolder
	local invoke = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
	invoke.Name = "Invoke"
	invoke.Parent = debugFolder
	invoke.OnInvoke = function(action: any, a: any, b: any)
		if action == "Inspect" then
			local snap = EconomyService.GetInspect(tostring(a))
			local encoded = if snap then HttpService:JSONEncode(snap) else "No economy state."
			print("[Economy] debug Inspect", encoded)
			return snap ~= nil, encoded
		elseif action == "SetGold" then
			local ok, result = EconomyService.DebugSetGold(tostring(a), tonumber(b) or -1)
			print("[Economy] debug SetGold", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "AddGold" then
			local ok, result = EconomyService.AddGold(tostring(a), tonumber(b) or -1)
			print("[Economy] debug AddGold", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "SpendGold" then
			local ok, result = EconomyService.TrySpend(tostring(a), tonumber(b) or -1)
			print("[Economy] debug SpendGold", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		end
		return false, "Unknown action."
	end

	Hierarchy.RulerCreated:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		ensureGold(rulerId)
		pushAllPlayers()
	end)
	Hierarchy.RulerRemoving:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		goldByRuler[rulerId] = nil
		pushAllPlayers()
	end)
	Hierarchy.StateChanged:Connect(function()
		pushAllPlayers()
	end)
	Territory.OwnershipChanged:Connect(function()
		pushAllPlayers()
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		task.defer(function()
			if player.Parent then
				pushPlayer(player)
			end
		end)
	end)

	runPayoutLoop()

	_G.__EconomyV01 = EconomyService
	print("[Economy] started. StartingGold", EconomyConfig.StartingGold, "interval", EconomyConfig.PayoutIntervalSeconds)
end

return EconomyService

-- Shared server-only gate. VictoryService alone ends the match.
local MatchState = {}
local final: any = nil

function MatchState.IsEnded(): boolean
	return final ~= nil
end

function MatchState.GetFinal(): any
	return final
end

function MatchState.End(snapshot: any): boolean
	if final then return false end
	final = snapshot
	return true
end

return MatchState

--[[
	Military / Armies V0.1 — Troops, commitments, battle, casualties.

	Does not SetOwner. Does not declare wars.
	Feeds WarfareService.ApplyMilitaryOutcome after presentation.
]]

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local MilitaryConfig = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.MilitaryConfig)
local MilitaryCombat = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.MilitaryCombat)
local Hierarchy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.PoliticalHierarchyService)
local Territory = require(ServerScriptService.Services.TerritoryMapService)
local Economy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.EconomyService)
local Warfare = require(ServerScriptService.Services.WarfareService)

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local Neutral = require(script.Parent.NeutralDefenseService)
local MilitaryService = {}

type SideState = {
	intended: number?,
	interacted: boolean,
	locked: boolean,
	lockedAmount: number?,
}

type Engagement = {
	warId: string,
	attackerRootId: string,
	defenderRootId: string,
	targetTerritoryId: string,
	startedAt: number,
	attacker: SideState,
	defender: SideState,
	mechanicallyResolved: boolean,
	presentationUntil: number?,
	battle: MilitaryCombat.BattleResult?,
	concluded: boolean,
	targetKind: string,
	targetRevision: number,
}

local started = false
local totalByRuler: { [string]: number } = {}
local reserved: { [string]: { [string]: number } } = {} -- warId -> rulerId -> amount
local engagements: { [string]: Engagement } = {}
local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?
local rngSeed: number? = nil
local diagEnabled = false

local function now(): number
	return workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end

local function diag(...)
	if diagEnabled then
		print("[Military:diag]", ...)
	end
end

local function findRuler(rulerId: string): any?
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.rulerId == rulerId then
			return ruler
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function territoryName(territoryId: string): string
	for _, t in Territory.GetMapDefinition().territories do
		if t.id == territoryId then
			if typeof(t.displayName) == "string" and t.displayName ~= "" then
				return t.displayName
			end
			return territoryId
		end
	end
	return territoryId
end

local function ensurePool(rulerId: string)
	if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		totalByRuler[rulerId] = MilitaryConfig.StartingTroops
	end
end

local function committedTotal(rulerId: string): number
	local sum = 0
	for _, byRuler in reserved do
		local n = byRuler[rulerId]
		if typeof(n) == "number" then
			sum += n
		end
	end
	return sum
end

local function availableOf(rulerId: string): number
	local total = totalByRuler[rulerId]
	if typeof(total) ~= "number" then
		return 0
	end
	return total - committedTotal(rulerId)
end

local function setReserved(warId: string, rulerId: string, amount: number)
	local row = reserved[warId]
	if row == nil then
		row = {}
		reserved[warId] = row
	end
	if amount <= 0 then
		row[rulerId] = nil
		if next(row) == nil then
			reserved[warId] = nil
		end
		return
	end
	row[rulerId] = amount
end

local function getReserved(warId: string, rulerId: string): number
	local row = reserved[warId]
	if not row then
		return 0
	end
	return row[rulerId] or 0
end

local function noticePlayer(player: Player, message: string)
	print("[Military] notice ->", player.Name, message)
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "Notice", message)
end

local function noticeRulerId(rulerId: string, message: string)
	local ruler = findRuler(rulerId)
	local userId = ruler and tonumber(ruler.userId)
	if typeof(userId) == "number" and userId ~= 0 then
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		if player then
			noticePlayer(player, message)
		end
	end
end

local function sideFor(eng: Engagement, rulerId: string): SideState?
	if rulerId == eng.attackerRootId then
		return eng.attacker
	elseif rulerId == eng.defenderRootId then
		return eng.defender
	end
	return nil
end

local function playerPayload(rulerId: string)
	local total = totalByRuler[rulerId] or 0
	local committed = committedTotal(rulerId)
	local wars = {}
	for _, eng in engagements do
		if eng.attackerRootId == rulerId or eng.defenderRootId == rulerId then
			local mine = sideFor(eng, rulerId) :: SideState
			local role = if rulerId == eng.attackerRootId then "Attacker" else "Defender"
			local bothLocked = eng.attacker.locked and eng.defender.locked
			local opponentState = if eng.targetKind == "Unclaimed" then "Militia ready — lock your commitment" else "Opponent preparing forces"
			if mine.locked and not bothLocked then
				opponentState = "Waiting for opponent"
			end
			local row: any = {
				warId = eng.warId,
				targetTerritoryId = eng.targetTerritoryId,
				targetDisplayName = territoryName(eng.targetTerritoryId),
				role = role,
				targetKind = eng.targetKind,
				neutralDefenders = if eng.targetKind == "Unclaimed" then eng.defender.lockedAmount else nil,
				intended = if mine.interacted then mine.intended else nil,
				locked = mine.locked,
				windowEndsAt = eng.startedAt + MilitaryConfig.CommitWindowSeconds,
				hardEndsAt = eng.startedAt + MilitaryConfig.HardTimeoutSeconds,
				opponentState = if bothLocked then nil else opponentState,
				phase = if eng.concluded
					then "done"
					elseif eng.mechanicallyResolved then "present"
					else "commit",
				presentationEndsAt = eng.presentationUntil,
			}
			if bothLocked and eng.battle then
				row.attackerCommitted = eng.battle.attackerCommitted
				row.defenderCommitted = eng.battle.defenderCommitted
			end
			table.insert(wars, row)
		end
	end
	table.sort(wars, function(a, b)
		return a.warId < b.warId
	end)
	return {
		total = total,
		available = availableOf(rulerId),
		committed = committed,
		troopsPerGold = MilitaryConfig.TroopsPerGold,
		wars = wars,
	}
end

local function inspectPayload(rulerId: string)
	local wars = {}
	for _, eng in engagements do
		if eng.attackerRootId == rulerId or eng.defenderRootId == rulerId then
			table.insert(wars, {
				warId = eng.warId,
				reserved = getReserved(eng.warId, rulerId),
				role = if rulerId == eng.attackerRootId then "Attacker" else "Defender",
				intended = if rulerId == eng.attackerRootId then eng.attacker.intended else eng.defender.intended,
				locked = if rulerId == eng.attackerRootId then eng.attacker.locked else eng.defender.locked,
				mechanicallyResolved = eng.mechanicallyResolved,
			})
		end
	end
	return {
		rulerId = rulerId,
		total = totalByRuler[rulerId] or 0,
		available = availableOf(rulerId),
		committed = committedTotal(rulerId),
		ledgerOk = (totalByRuler[rulerId] or 0) == availableOf(rulerId) + committedTotal(rulerId),
		wars = wars,
	}
end

local function pushPlayer(player: Player)
	if not started then
		return
	end
	local rulerId = Hierarchy.GetRulerIdForUserId(player.UserId)
	local payload
	if rulerId and totalByRuler[rulerId] ~= nil then
		payload = playerPayload(rulerId)
	else
		payload = {
			total = 0,
			available = 0,
			committed = 0,
			troopsPerGold = MilitaryConfig.TroopsPerGold,
			wars = {},
		}
	end
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "State", HttpService:JSONEncode(payload))
end

local function pushAllPlayers()
	if debugSnapshot then
		local rows = {}
		for rulerId, total in totalByRuler do
			table.insert(rows, {
				rulerId = rulerId,
				total = total,
				available = availableOf(rulerId),
				committed = committedTotal(rulerId),
			})
		end
		debugSnapshot.Value = HttpService:JSONEncode({
			rulers = rows,
			engagements = (function()
				local list = {}
				for id, eng in engagements do
					table.insert(list, {
						warId = id,
						mechanical = eng.mechanicallyResolved,
						atkLocked = eng.attacker.locked,
						defLocked = eng.defender.locked,
					})
				end
				return list
			end)(),
		})
	end
	for _, player in Players:GetPlayers() do
		pushPlayer(player)
	end
end

local function makeRng(): Random
	if rngSeed ~= nil then
		return Random.new(rngSeed)
	end
	return Random.new()
end

local function applyCasualties(eng: Engagement, battle: MilitaryCombat.BattleResult)
	local function apply(rulerId: string, committed: number, casualties: number)
		if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
			return
		end
		local survivors = math.max(0, committed - casualties)
		totalByRuler[rulerId] = math.max(0, totalByRuler[rulerId] - casualties)
		setReserved(eng.warId, rulerId, survivors)
	end
	apply(eng.attackerRootId, battle.attackerCommitted, battle.attackerCasualties)
	apply(eng.defenderRootId, battle.defenderCommitted, battle.defenderCasualties)
end

local function returnSurvivors(eng: Engagement)
	setReserved(eng.warId, eng.attackerRootId, 0)
	setReserved(eng.warId, eng.defenderRootId, 0)
	reserved[eng.warId] = nil
end

local function pushBattleResult(rulerId: string, payload: any)
	local ruler = findRuler(rulerId)
	local userId = ruler and tonumber(ruler.userId)
	if typeof(userId) ~= "number" or userId == 0 then
		return
	end
	local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
	if player then
		serverPush:FireClient(player, "BattleResult", HttpService:JSONEncode(payload))
	end
end

local function concludeEngagement(eng: Engagement)
	if eng.concluded then
		return
	end
	eng.concluded = true
	local battle = eng.battle
	if battle then
		local function resultFor(role: string)
			local won = (role == "Attacker" and battle.winner == "Attacker") or (role == "Defender" and battle.winner == "Defender")
			local title
			local attacker = findRuler(eng.attackerRootId)
			local staleNeutral = eng.targetKind == "Unclaimed" and (not Warfare.IsTargetCurrent(eng.warId) or not attacker or not attacker.independent)
			if staleNeutral and role == "Attacker" then
				title = "BATTLE ENDED — TARGET NO LONGER AVAILABLE"
			elseif battle.unopposed then
				title = "UNOPPOSED — " .. string.upper(territoryName(eng.targetTerritoryId)) .. " TAKEN"
			elseif won and role == "Attacker" then
				title = "VICTORY — " .. string.upper(territoryName(eng.targetTerritoryId)) .. " CAPTURED"
			elseif won then
				title = "DEFENDED — " .. string.upper(territoryName(eng.targetTerritoryId)) .. " HELD"
			else
				title = "DEFEAT — " .. string.upper(territoryName(eng.targetTerritoryId))
			end
			local myCommit = if role == "Attacker" then battle.attackerCommitted else battle.defenderCommitted
			local myCas = if role == "Attacker" then battle.attackerCasualties else battle.defenderCasualties
			local mySurv = if role == "Attacker" then battle.attackerSurvivors else battle.defenderSurvivors
			local enemyCommit = if role == "Attacker" then battle.defenderCommitted else battle.attackerCommitted
			return {
				title = title,
				committed = myCommit,
				enemyCommitted = enemyCommit,
				casualties = myCas,
				survivors = mySurv,
				unopposed = battle.unopposed,
				won = won,
				warId = eng.warId,
				targetTerritoryId = eng.targetTerritoryId,
				targetDisplayName = territoryName(eng.targetTerritoryId),
			}
		end
		pushBattleResult(eng.attackerRootId, resultFor("Attacker"))
		pushBattleResult(eng.defenderRootId, resultFor("Defender"))
	end
	returnSurvivors(eng)
	if battle then
		local outcome = battle.winner
		local ok, err = Warfare.ApplyMilitaryOutcome(eng.warId, outcome)
		print("[Military] ApplyMilitaryOutcome", eng.warId, outcome, ok, err)
	end
	engagements[eng.warId] = nil
	pushAllPlayers()
end

local function cancelEngagement(eng: Engagement, reason: string)
	if eng.mechanicallyResolved then
		return
	end
	returnSurvivors(eng)
	engagements[eng.warId] = nil
	Warfare.CancelWar(eng.warId, reason)
	pushAllPlayers()
end

local function resolveBattle(eng: Engagement)
	if eng.mechanicallyResolved then
		return
	end
	local atk = eng.attacker.lockedAmount
	local def = eng.defender.lockedAmount
	if typeof(atk) ~= "number" or typeof(def) ~= "number" then
		return
	end
	if atk <= 0 then
		diag("attacker locked 0; cancel", eng.warId)
		cancelEngagement(eng, "attacker committed no force")
		noticeRulerId(eng.attackerRootId, "Attack cancelled: you committed 0 Troops.")
		return
	end
	if eng.targetKind == "Unclaimed" and (not Warfare.IsTargetCurrent(eng.warId) or not (findRuler(eng.attackerRootId) and findRuler(eng.attackerRootId).independent)) then
		cancelEngagement(eng, "stale target")
		return
	end
	local battle = MilitaryCombat.Resolve(atk, def, makeRng())
	eng.battle = battle
	eng.mechanicallyResolved = true
	eng.presentationUntil = now() + MilitaryConfig.PresentationSeconds
	applyCasualties(eng, battle)
	if eng.targetKind == "Unclaimed" then Neutral.ApplySurvivors(eng.targetTerritoryId, eng.targetRevision, battle.defenderSurvivors) end
	diag("battle resolved", eng.warId, battle.winner, battle.band or "unopposed")
	pushAllPlayers()
end

local function tryLock(eng: Engagement, side: SideState, amount: number)
	if side.locked then
		return
	end
	side.locked = true
	side.lockedAmount = amount
	side.intended = amount
	side.interacted = true
	if side == eng.attacker and amount <= 0 then
		diag("attacker locked 0; cancel", eng.warId)
		cancelEngagement(eng, "attacker committed no force")
		noticeRulerId(eng.attackerRootId, "Attack cancelled: you committed 0 Troops.")
		return
	end
	if eng.attacker.locked and eng.defender.locked then
		resolveBattle(eng)
	else
		pushAllPlayers()
	end
end

function MilitaryService.FreezeMatch()
	for _, eng in engagements do returnSurvivors(eng) end
	table.clear(engagements)
	table.clear(reserved)
	pushAllPlayers()
end

function MilitaryService.IsMechanicallyResolved(warId: string): boolean
	local eng = engagements[warId]
	return eng ~= nil and eng.mechanicallyResolved == true
end

function MilitaryService.UnwindPreResolution(warId: string): (boolean, string)
	local eng = engagements[warId]
	if not eng then
		return true, "No military engagement."
	end
	if eng.mechanicallyResolved then
		return false, "War is already resolving."
	end
	returnSurvivors(eng)
	engagements[warId] = nil
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, "Military commitments unwound."
end

function MilitaryService.OnWarCreated(war: any)
	if typeof(war) ~= "table" or typeof(war.warId) ~= "string" then
		return
	end
	if engagements[war.warId] then
		return
	end
	engagements[war.warId] = {
		warId = war.warId,
		attackerRootId = war.attackerRootId,
		defenderRootId = war.defenderRootId,
		targetTerritoryId = war.targetTerritoryId,
		startedAt = now(),
		attacker = { intended = nil, interacted = false, locked = false, lockedAmount = nil },
		defender = if war.targetKind == "Unclaimed"
			then { intended = war.neutralDefenders, interacted = true, locked = true, lockedAmount = war.neutralDefenders }
			else { intended = nil, interacted = false, locked = false, lockedAmount = nil },
		targetKind = war.targetKind or "Player",
		targetRevision = war.targetRevision or 0,
		mechanicallyResolved = false,
		presentationUntil = nil,
		battle = nil,
		concluded = false,
	}
	pushAllPlayers()
end

function MilitaryService.Recruit(rulerId: string, troopAmount: any): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No troop state."
	end
	if typeof(troopAmount) ~= "number" or troopAmount ~= troopAmount then
		return false, "Recruit amount must be a whole number."
	end
	if troopAmount ~= math.floor(troopAmount) then
		return false, "Recruit amount must be a whole number."
	end
	if troopAmount <= 0 then
		return false, "Recruit amount must be greater than 0."
	end
	if troopAmount < MilitaryConfig.TroopsPerGold then
		return false, "Minimum recruit is 3 Troops."
	end
	if troopAmount % MilitaryConfig.TroopsPerGold ~= 0 then
		return false, "Recruit amount must be a multiple of 3 Troops."
	end
	local goldCost = troopAmount / MilitaryConfig.TroopsPerGold
	local ok, err = Economy.TrySpend(rulerId, goldCost)
	if not ok then
		return false, err
	end
	totalByRuler[rulerId] += troopAmount
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Recruited %d Troops for %d Gold.", troopAmount, goldCost)
end

function MilitaryService.RecruitMax(rulerId: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No troop state."
	end
	local gold = Economy.GetBalance(rulerId)
	if typeof(gold) ~= "number" or gold ~= gold or gold < 1 then
		return false, "Insufficient Gold."
	end
	local wholeGold = math.floor(gold)
	if wholeGold < 1 then
		return false, "Insufficient Gold."
	end
	return MilitaryService.Recruit(rulerId, wholeGold * MilitaryConfig.TroopsPerGold)
end

function MilitaryService.SetCommit(rulerId: string, warId: string, amountAny: any): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local eng = engagements[warId]
	if not eng or eng.concluded then
		return false, "No active engagement."
	end
	if eng.mechanicallyResolved then
		return false, "Battle is already resolving."
	end
	local side = sideFor(eng, rulerId)
	if not side then
		return false, "Only the war roots may commit Troops."
	end
	if side.locked then
		return false, "Commitment is locked."
	end
	if typeof(amountAny) ~= "number" or amountAny ~= amountAny then
		return false, "Commitment must be a whole number."
	end
	if amountAny ~= math.floor(amountAny) then
		return false, "Commitment must be a whole number."
	end
	if amountAny < 0 then
		return false, "Commitment cannot be negative."
	end
	local current = getReserved(warId, rulerId)
	local available = availableOf(rulerId) + current
	if amountAny > available then
		return false, "Not enough Available Troops."
	end
	if side.interacted == true and side.intended == amountAny and current == amountAny then
		return true, string.format("Commitment set to %d.", amountAny)
	end
	setReserved(warId, rulerId, amountAny)
	side.intended = amountAny
	side.interacted = true
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Commitment set to %d.", amountAny)
end

function MilitaryService.LockCommit(rulerId: string, warId: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local eng = engagements[warId]
	if not eng or eng.concluded then
		return false, "No active engagement."
	end
	if eng.mechanicallyResolved then
		return false, "Battle is already resolving."
	end
	local side = sideFor(eng, rulerId)
	if not side then
		return false, "Only the war roots may lock."
	end
	if side.locked then
		return false, "Already locked."
	end
	if not side.interacted or typeof(side.intended) ~= "number" then
		return false, "Submit a commitment first."
	end
	local amount = side.intended
	tryLock(eng, side, amount)
	if side == eng.attacker and amount <= 0 then
		return true, "Attack cancelled: you committed 0 Troops."
	end
	return true, "Forces locked."
end

function MilitaryService.GetInspect(rulerId: string)
	if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return nil
	end
	return inspectPayload(rulerId)
end

function MilitaryService.GetEngagementInspect(warId: string)
	local eng = engagements[warId]
	if not eng then
		return nil
	end
	return {
		warId = eng.warId,
		targetTerritoryId = eng.targetTerritoryId,
		startedAt = eng.startedAt,
		now = now(),
		windowEndsAt = eng.startedAt + MilitaryConfig.CommitWindowSeconds,
		hardEndsAt = eng.startedAt + MilitaryConfig.HardTimeoutSeconds,
		attacker = {
			rulerId = eng.attackerRootId,
			intended = eng.attacker.intended,
			interacted = eng.attacker.interacted,
			locked = eng.attacker.locked,
			lockedAmount = eng.attacker.lockedAmount,
			reserved = getReserved(eng.warId, eng.attackerRootId),
		},
		defender = {
			rulerId = eng.defenderRootId,
			intended = eng.defender.intended,
			interacted = eng.defender.interacted,
			locked = eng.defender.locked,
			lockedAmount = eng.defender.lockedAmount,
			reserved = getReserved(eng.warId, eng.defenderRootId),
		},
		mechanicallyResolved = eng.mechanicallyResolved,
		presentationUntil = eng.presentationUntil,
		battle = eng.battle,
	}
end

function MilitaryService.DebugSetTroops(rulerId: string, value: number): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No troop state."
	end
	if typeof(value) ~= "number" or value ~= value or value ~= math.floor(value) or value < 0 then
		return false, "Troops must be a whole number >= 0."
	end
	local committed = committedTotal(rulerId)
	if value < committed then
		return false, "Cannot set Total below currently committed Troops."
	end
	totalByRuler[rulerId] = value
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Troops set to %d.", value)
end

function MilitaryService.DebugAddTroops(rulerId: string, value: number): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if totalByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		return false, "No troop state."
	end
	if typeof(value) ~= "number" or value ~= value or value ~= math.floor(value) then
		return false, "Need a whole number."
	end
	local nextTotal = totalByRuler[rulerId] + value
	if nextTotal < committedTotal(rulerId) then
		return false, "Cannot reduce Total below currently committed Troops."
	end
	if nextTotal < 0 then
		return false, "Troops cannot be negative."
	end
	totalByRuler[rulerId] = nextTotal
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, string.format("Troops now %d.", nextTotal)
end

function MilitaryService.DebugSetSeed(seed: number): (boolean, string)
	if typeof(seed) ~= "number" then
		rngSeed = nil
		return true, "RNG seed cleared."
	end
	rngSeed = math.floor(seed)
	return true, "RNG seed set."
end

function MilitaryService.DebugSetDiag(enabled: boolean): (boolean, string)
	diagEnabled = enabled == true
	return true, if diagEnabled then "Diagnostics on." else "Diagnostics off."
end

local function stillOpen(eng: Engagement): boolean
	return engagements[eng.warId] == eng and not eng.concluded and not eng.mechanicallyResolved
end

local function tickEngagements()
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return end
	local t = now()
	local snapshot = {}
	for _, eng in engagements do
		table.insert(snapshot, eng)
	end
	for _, eng in snapshot do
		if MatchState.IsEnded() then break end
		if not eng.concluded then
			if eng.mechanicallyResolved then
				if eng.presentationUntil and t >= eng.presentationUntil then
					concludeEngagement(eng)
				end
			else
				if t >= eng.startedAt + MilitaryConfig.CommitWindowSeconds then
					if not eng.attacker.locked and eng.attacker.interacted and typeof(eng.attacker.intended) == "number" then
						tryLock(eng, eng.attacker, eng.attacker.intended)
					end
					if stillOpen(eng) and not eng.defender.locked and eng.defender.interacted and typeof(eng.defender.intended) == "number" then
						tryLock(eng, eng.defender, eng.defender.intended)
					end
				end
				if stillOpen(eng) and t >= eng.startedAt + MilitaryConfig.HardTimeoutSeconds then
					if not eng.attacker.locked then
						cancelEngagement(eng, "attacker did not commit")
					elseif not eng.defender.locked then
						tryLock(eng, eng.defender, 0)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function MilitaryService.DebugAdvance(seconds: number): (boolean, string)
	if typeof(seconds) ~= "number" or seconds ~= seconds then
		return false, "Need seconds."
	end
	for _, eng in engagements do
		eng.startedAt -= seconds
		if eng.presentationUntil then
			eng.presentationUntil -= seconds
		end
	end
	tickEngagements()
	pushAllPlayers()
	return true, "Advanced military timers."
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any, a: any, b: any)
	if typeof(action) ~= "string" then
		noticePlayer(player, "Invalid request.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Ready" then
		pushPlayer(player)
		return
	end
	local fromId = Hierarchy.GetRulerIdForUserId(player.UserId)
	if not fromId then
		noticePlayer(player, "No ruler record.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Recruit" then
		local ok, result = MilitaryService.Recruit(fromId, a)
		noticePlayer(player, result)
		if not ok then
			return
		end
		return
	end
	if action == "RecruitMax" then
		local ok, result = MilitaryService.RecruitMax(fromId)
		noticePlayer(player, result)
		return
	end
	if action == "SetCommit" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			noticePlayer(player, "Invalid war.")
			return
		end
		local ok, result = MilitaryService.SetCommit(fromId, a, b)
		if not ok then
			noticePlayer(player, result)
		end
		return
	end
	if action == "LockCommit" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			noticePlayer(player, "Invalid war.")
			return
		end
		local ok, result = MilitaryService.LockCommit(fromId, a)
		noticePlayer(player, result)
		return
	end
	noticePlayer(player, "Unknown action.")
end

function MilitaryService.Start()
	if started then
		return
	end
	started = true

	local remotesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes") :: Folder
	clientIntent = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("MilitaryClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	serverPush = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("MilitaryServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	clientIntent.OnServerEvent:Connect(onClientIntent)

	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		ensurePool(ruler.rulerId)
	end

	local debugFolder = ServerScriptService:FindFirstChild("MilitaryDebug")
	if debugFolder then
		debugFolder:Destroy()
	end
	debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "MilitaryDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = ServerScriptService
	local help = Instance.new("StringValue")
	help.Name = "Help"
	help.Value =
		"Invoke('Inspect'| 'SetTroops'| 'AddTroops'| 'InspectWar'| 'SelfTest'| 'SetSeed'| 'SetDiag', ...). Prefer AdminBridge/Kohl."
	help.Parent = debugFolder
	debugSnapshot = Instance.new("StringValue")
	debugSnapshot.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	debugSnapshot.Parent = debugFolder
	local invoke = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
	invoke.Name = "Invoke"
	invoke.Parent = debugFolder
	invoke.OnInvoke = function(action: any, a: any, b: any, c: any)
		if action == "Inspect" then
			local snap = MilitaryService.GetInspect(tostring(a))
			local encoded = if snap then HttpService:JSONEncode(snap) else "No troop state."
			print("[Military] debug Inspect", encoded)
			return snap ~= nil, encoded
		elseif action == "InspectWar" then
			local snap = MilitaryService.GetEngagementInspect(tostring(a))
			local encoded = if snap then HttpService:JSONEncode(snap) else "No engagement."
			print("[Military] debug InspectWar", encoded)
			return snap ~= nil, encoded
		elseif action == "SetTroops" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.DebugSetTroops(tostring(a), tonumber(b) or -1)
			print("[Military] debug SetTroops", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "AddTroops" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.DebugAddTroops(tostring(a), tonumber(b) or 0)
			print("[Military] debug AddTroops", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "SetSeed" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.DebugSetSeed(tonumber(a) or 0)
			print("[Military] debug SetSeed", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "SetDiag" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.DebugSetDiag(a == true or a == "true" or a == 1)
			print("[Military] debug SetDiag", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "SelfTest" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryCombat.SelfTest()
			print("[Military] SelfTest", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Advance" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.DebugAdvance(tonumber(a) or 0)
			print("[Military] debug Advance", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Recruit" then
			local amount = if typeof(b) == "number" then b else b
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.Recruit(tostring(a), amount)
			print("[Military] debug Recruit", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "RecruitMax" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.RecruitMax(tostring(a))
			print("[Military] debug RecruitMax", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "SetCommit" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.SetCommit(tostring(a), tostring(b), c)
			print("[Military] debug SetCommit", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "LockCommit" then
			local ok, result = MilitaryService.LockCommit(tostring(a), tostring(b))
			print("[Military] debug LockCommit", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		end
		return false, "Unknown action."
	end

	_G.__MilitaryV01 = MilitaryService

	Hierarchy.RulerCreated:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		ensurePool(rulerId)
		pushAllPlayers()
	end)
	Hierarchy.RulerRemoving:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		totalByRuler[rulerId] = nil
		for warId, byRuler in reserved do
			byRuler[rulerId] = nil
			if next(byRuler) == nil then
				reserved[warId] = nil
			end
		end
		pushAllPlayers()
	end)

	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(tickEngagements)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		task.defer(function()
			if player.Parent then
				pushPlayer(player)
			end
		end)
	end)

	pushAllPlayers()
	print("[Military] service started")
end

return MilitaryService

-- Territory-owned militia, never a ruler or player Troop pool.
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.NeutralDefenseConfig)
local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local Service = {}
local defenses: { [string]: { current: number, maximum: number, revision: number } } = {}

function Service.Get(territoryId: string): any
	local Territory = require(script.Parent.TerritoryMapService)
	if Territory.GetOwnerRulerId(territoryId) then return nil end
	local revision = Territory.GetOwnershipRevision(territoryId)
	local row = defenses[territoryId]
	if row and row.revision == revision then return table.clone(row) end
	local map = Territory.GetMapDefinition()
	for _, definition in map.territories do
		if definition.id == territoryId then
			local maximum = definition.unclaimedDefenders or map.unclaimedDefenders or Config.BaseDefenders
			assert(typeof(maximum) == "number" and maximum == math.floor(maximum) and maximum >= 0 and maximum < math.huge, "Invalid Unclaimed defense config")
			row = { current = maximum, maximum = maximum, revision = revision }
			defenses[territoryId] = row
			return table.clone(row)
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function Service.ApplySurvivors(territoryId: string, revision: number, survivors: number): boolean
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false end
	local row = Service.Get(territoryId)
	if not row or row.revision ~= revision then return false end
	if typeof(survivors) ~= "number" or survivors ~= math.floor(survivors) or survivors < 0 or survivors > row.current then return false end
	defenses[territoryId].current = survivors
	require(script.Parent.TerritoryMapService).RefreshPublicState()
	return true
end

function Service.DebugSet(territoryId: string, amount: any): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local row = Service.Get(territoryId)
	if not row then return false, "Target must be Unclaimed." end
	local Warfare = require(script.Parent.WarfareService)
	for _, war in Warfare.GetSnapshot().wars do
		if war.targetTerritoryId == territoryId then return false, "Territory is contested." end
	end
	if typeof(amount) ~= "number" or amount ~= math.floor(amount) or amount < 0 or amount > row.maximum then return false, "Defense must be a whole number within its configured maximum." end
	defenses[territoryId].current = amount
	require(script.Parent.TerritoryMapService).RefreshPublicState()
	return true, "Unclaimed defense updated."
end

return Service
