assert(game:GetService("ServerScriptService"):WaitForChild("Services"):WaitForChild("PoliticalHierarchyService").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

--[[
	Authoritative political hierarchy for V0.1.

	Human players become Independent Lords on join.
	Voluntary vassalage requires both parties and server-side validation.
	Subordinate trees are preserved (only the joining ruler's liege changes).
	Disconnect cleanup is temporary prototype behavior: the leaving ruler's
	runtime record is removed; remaining trees stay valid. No AI replacement,
	reconnection, succession, or persistence.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.PoliticalHierarchyConfig)
local RankRules = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.PoliticalRankRules)

type Ruler = {
	rulerId: string,
	userId: number?,
	player: Player?,
	displayName: string,
	titleRank: number,
	liegeId: string?,
	vassalIds: { [string]: boolean },
	isMock: boolean,
}

type FealtyRequest = {
	requestId: string,
	fromRulerId: string,
	toRulerId: string,
	createdAt: number,
	expiresAt: number,
	expireThread: thread?,
}

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local PoliticalHierarchyService = {}

local started = false
local rulersById: { [string]: Ruler } = {}
local rulerIdByUserId: { [number]: string } = {}
local requestsById: { [string]: FealtyRequest } = {}

local remotesFolder: Folder
local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?

local rulerCreatedBindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local rulerRemovingBindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local stateChangedBindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
PoliticalHierarchyService.RulerCreated = rulerCreatedBindable.Event
PoliticalHierarchyService.RulerRemoving = rulerRemovingBindable.Event
PoliticalHierarchyService.StateChanged = stateChangedBindable.Event

local function now(): number
	return os.clock()
end

local function listKeys(set: { [string]: boolean }): { string }
	local list = {}
	for id in set do
		table.insert(list, id)
	end
	table.sort(list)
	return list
end

local function collectDescendants(root: Ruler): { string }
	local result = {}
	local queue = { root.rulerId }
	local seen: { [string]: boolean } = { [root.rulerId] = true }
	local i = 1
	while i <= #queue do
		local current = rulersById[queue[i]]
		i += 1
		if current then
			for vassalId in current.vassalIds do
				if not seen[vassalId] then
					seen[vassalId] = true
					table.insert(result, vassalId)
					table.insert(queue, vassalId)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(result)
	return result
end

local function isInSubtree(rootId: string, maybeDescendantId: string): boolean
	if rootId == maybeDescendantId then
		return true
	end
	local root = rulersById[rootId]
	if not root then
		return false
	end
	for _, id in collectDescendants(root) do
		if id == maybeDescendantId then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function getRootId(rulerId: string): string
	local current = rulersById[rulerId]
	local guard = 0
	while current and current.liegeId and guard < 64 do
		local liege = rulersById[current.liegeId]
		if not liege then
			break
		end
		current = liege
		guard += 1
	end
	return if current then current.rulerId else rulerId
end

local function snapshotRuler(ruler: Ruler)
	-- Avoid nil fields: RemoteEvents can drop table payloads in multi-client Studio.
	return {
		rulerId = ruler.rulerId,
		userId = ruler.userId or 0,
		hasUser = ruler.userId ~= nil,
		displayName = ruler.displayName,
		titleRank = ruler.titleRank,
		titleName = RankRules.Name(ruler.titleRank),
		liegeId = ruler.liegeId or "",
		independent = ruler.liegeId == nil,
		vassalIds = listKeys(ruler.vassalIds),
		descendantIds = collectDescendants(ruler),
		rootRulerId = getRootId(ruler.rulerId),
	}
end

local function buildSnapshot()
	local rulers = {}
	for _, ruler in rulersById do
		table.insert(rulers, snapshotRuler(ruler))
	end
	table.sort(rulers, function(a, b)
		return a.displayName < b.displayName
	end)
	local requests = {}
	local t = now()
	for _, request in requestsById do
		if request.expiresAt > t then
			table.insert(requests, {
				requestId = request.requestId,
				fromRulerId = request.fromRulerId,
				toRulerId = request.toRulerId,
				expiresAt = request.expiresAt,
			})
		end
	end
	return {
		rulers = rulers,
		requests = requests,
	}
end

local function pushState(toPlayer: Player?)
	local payload = buildSnapshot()
	local encoded = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
	if debugSnapshot then
		debugSnapshot.Value = encoded
	end
	local n = #payload.rulers
	if toPlayer then
		print("[Hierarchy] push State to", toPlayer.Name, "rulers=", n, "requests=", #payload.requests)
		serverPush:FireClient(toPlayer, "State", encoded)
	else
		print("[Hierarchy] push State all clients rulers=", n, "requests=", #payload.requests)
		serverPush:FireAllClients("State", encoded)
	end
	stateChangedBindable:Fire()
	require(script.Parent.VictoryService).Evaluate()
end

local function notice(player: Player, message: string)
	print("[Hierarchy] notice ->", player.Name, message)
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "Notice", message)
end

local function noticeRuler(ruler: Ruler?, message: string)
	local target = ruler and ruler.player
	if target and target.Parent then
		notice(target, message)
	end
end

local function cancelExpire(request: FealtyRequest)
	local thread = request.expireThread
	request.expireThread = nil
	if thread then
		pcall(function()
			task.cancel(thread)
		end)
	end
end

local function removeRequest(requestId: string)
	local request = requestsById[requestId]
	if not request then
		return
	end
	cancelExpire(request)
	requestsById[requestId] = nil
end

local function supersedePair(fromRulerId: string, toRulerId: string)
	for id, request in requestsById do
		if request.fromRulerId == fromRulerId and request.toRulerId == toRulerId then
			removeRequest(id)
		end
	end
end

local function detachFromLiege(ruler: Ruler)
	local liegeId = ruler.liegeId
	if not liegeId then
		return
	end
	local liege = rulersById[liegeId]
	if liege then
		liege.vassalIds[ruler.rulerId] = nil
	end
	ruler.liegeId = nil
end

local function attachVassal(liege: Ruler, vassal: Ruler)
	detachFromLiege(vassal)
	vassal.liegeId = liege.rulerId
	liege.vassalIds[vassal.rulerId] = true
end

local function removeRulerInternal(rulerId: string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return end
	local ruler = rulersById[rulerId]
	if not ruler then
		return
	end
	rulerRemovingBindable:Fire(ruler.rulerId)
	detachFromLiege(ruler)
	for vassalId in ruler.vassalIds do
		local vassal = rulersById[vassalId]
		if vassal then
			vassal.liegeId = nil
		end
	end
	for requestId, request in requestsById do
		if request.fromRulerId == rulerId or request.toRulerId == rulerId then
			removeRequest(requestId)
		end
	end
	if ruler.userId then
		rulerIdByUserId[ruler.userId] = nil
	end
	rulersById[rulerId] = nil
end

local function createRuler(props: {
	rulerId: string,
	userId: number?,
	player: Player?,
	displayName: string,
	titleRank: number,
	isMock: boolean,
}): Ruler
	local ruler: Ruler = {
		rulerId = props.rulerId,
		userId = props.userId,
		player = props.player,
		displayName = props.displayName,
		titleRank = props.titleRank,
		liegeId = nil,
		vassalIds = {},
		isMock = props.isMock,
	}
	rulersById[ruler.rulerId] = ruler
	if ruler.userId then
		rulerIdByUserId[ruler.userId] = ruler.rulerId
	end
	print("[Hierarchy] ruler created", ruler.displayName, "userId=", tostring(ruler.userId), "id=", ruler.rulerId)
	rulerCreatedBindable:Fire(ruler.rulerId, ruler.userId)
	return ruler
end

local function validateFealty(fromRulerId: string, toRulerId: string): (boolean, string)
	if fromRulerId == toRulerId then
		return false, "Cannot offer vassalage to yourself."
	end
	local fromRuler = rulersById[fromRulerId]
	local toRuler = rulersById[toRulerId]
	if not fromRuler or not toRuler then
		return false, "Ruler no longer exists."
	end
	if fromRuler.liegeId == toRulerId then
		return false, "That direct relationship already exists."
	end
	if not RankRules.CanBecomeVassalOf(fromRuler.titleRank, toRuler.titleRank) then
		return false, "Cannot become the vassal of a lower-ranked ruler."
	end
	if isInSubtree(fromRulerId, toRulerId) then
		return false, "That change would create a political cycle."
	end
	return true, ""
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.OfferFealty(fromRulerId: string, toRulerId: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local ok, err = validateFealty(fromRulerId, toRulerId)
	if not ok then
		return false, err
	end
	supersedePair(fromRulerId, toRulerId)
	local requestId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	local created = now()
	local lifetime = Config.VassalageRequestLifetimeSeconds
	if typeof(lifetime) ~= "number" or lifetime <= 0 then
		lifetime = 30
	end
	local request: FealtyRequest = {
		requestId = requestId,
		fromRulerId = fromRulerId,
		toRulerId = toRulerId,
		createdAt = created,
		expiresAt = created + lifetime,
		expireThread = nil,
	}
	request.expireThread = task.delay(lifetime, function()
		local expired = requestsById[requestId]
		if expired then
			expired.expireThread = nil
			local fromRuler = rulersById[expired.fromRulerId]
			removeRequest(requestId)
			pushState()
			noticeRuler(fromRuler, "Request expired.")
		end
	end)
	requestsById[requestId] = request
	pushState()
	return true, requestId
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.DeclineFealty(requestId: string, actorRulerId: string?): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local request = requestsById[requestId]
	if not request then
		return false, "Request is no longer valid."
	end
	if now() >= request.expiresAt then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, "Request has expired."
	end
	if actorRulerId and actorRulerId ~= request.toRulerId then
		return false, "Only the prospective liege may decline this request."
	end
	local fromRuler = rulersById[request.fromRulerId]
	local toRuler = rulersById[request.toRulerId]
	removeRequest(requestId)
	pushState()
	if toRuler then
		noticeRuler(toRuler, "Vassalage declined.")
	end
	if fromRuler and toRuler then
		noticeRuler(fromRuler, string.format("%s declined your vassalage offer.", toRuler.displayName))
	end
	return true, "Vassalage declined."
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.AcceptFealty(requestId: string, actorRulerId: string?): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local request = requestsById[requestId]
	if not request then
		return false, "Request is no longer valid."
	end
	if now() >= request.expiresAt then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, "Request has expired."
	end
	if actorRulerId and actorRulerId ~= request.toRulerId then
		return false, "Only the prospective liege may accept this request."
	end
	local ok, err = validateFealty(request.fromRulerId, request.toRulerId)
	if not ok then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, err
	end
	local fromRuler = rulersById[request.fromRulerId]
	local toRuler = rulersById[request.toRulerId]
	if not fromRuler or not toRuler then
		removeRequest(requestId)
		pushState()
		return false, "Ruler no longer exists."
	end
	attachVassal(toRuler, fromRuler)
	removeRequest(requestId)
	pushState()
	noticeRuler(toRuler, "Vassalage accepted.")
	noticeRuler(fromRuler, string.format("%s accepted your vassalage offer.", toRuler.displayName))
	return true, "Vassalage accepted."
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.GetSnapshot()
	return buildSnapshot()
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.GetRulerIdForUserId(userId: number): string?
	return rulerIdByUserId[userId]
end

-- Production rank mutation used by Title Progression after server revalidation.
-- Does not change liege/vassal links or territory.
function PoliticalHierarchyService.SetTitleRank(rulerId: string, titleRank: number): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if not RankRules.IsValidRank(titleRank) then
		return false, "Invalid rank."
	end
	local ruler = rulersById[rulerId]
	if not ruler then
		return false, "Ruler no longer exists."
	end
	if ruler.titleRank == titleRank then
		return true, "Rank unchanged."
	end
	ruler.titleRank = titleRank
	print("[Hierarchy] rank set", ruler.displayName, RankRules.Name(titleRank))
	pushState()
	return true, "Rank updated."
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.DebugCreateMockRuler(displayName: string, titleRank: number): string
	assert(RankRules.IsValidRank(titleRank), "Invalid rank")
	local ruler = createRuler({
		rulerId = "Mock_" .. HttpService:GenerateGUID(false),
		displayName = displayName,
		titleRank = titleRank,
		isMock = true,
	})
	pushState()
	return ruler.rulerId
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.DebugSetRank(rulerId: string, titleRank: number)
	local ok, err = PoliticalHierarchyService.SetTitleRank(rulerId, titleRank)
	assert(ok, err)
end

function PoliticalHierarchyService.DebugRemoveRuler(rulerId: string)
	removeRulerInternal(rulerId)
	pushState()
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any, a: any, b: any)
	if typeof(action) ~= "string" then
		notice(player, "Invalid request.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Ready" then
		print("[Hierarchy] Ready from", player.Name)
		pushState(player)
		return
	end
	local fromId = rulerIdByUserId[player.UserId]
	if not fromId then
		notice(player, "No ruler record.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Offer" then
		local targetUserId = tonumber(a)
		if typeof(targetUserId) ~= "number" then
			notice(player, "Invalid target.")
			return
		end
		local toId = rulerIdByUserId[targetUserId]
		if not toId then
			notice(player, "Target ruler not found.")
			return
		end
		local ok, result = PoliticalHierarchyService.OfferFealty(fromId, toId)
		if not ok then
			notice(player, result)
		else
			notice(player, "Vassalage offered.")
		end
		return
	end
	if action == "Accept" or action == "Decline" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			notice(player, "Invalid request.")
			return
		end
		local ok, result
		if action == "Accept" then
			ok, result = PoliticalHierarchyService.AcceptFealty(a, fromId)
		else
			ok, result = PoliticalHierarchyService.DeclineFealty(a, fromId)
		end
		if not ok then
			notice(player, result)
		end
		return
	end
	notice(player, "Unknown action.")
end

local function onPlayerAdded(player: Player)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then pushState(player); return end
	local existing = rulerIdByUserId[player.UserId]
	if existing then
		removeRulerInternal(existing)
	end
	createRuler({
		rulerId = "Player_" .. tostring(player.UserId),
		userId = player.UserId,
		player = player,
		displayName = player.DisplayName,
		titleRank = RankRules.Lord,
		isMock = false,
	})

end

	if rulerId then
		-- Temporary V0.1 disconnect: drop the runtime ruler; do not replace with AI.
		removeRulerInternal(rulerId)
		pushState()
	end
end

		return

	started = true

		debugFolder:Destroy()

			PoliticalHierarchyService.DebugCreateMockRuler(name, rank)

		local raw = debugFolder:GetAttribute("Offer")

			return
		end

			return
		end

--[[
	Authoritative territory ownership for Territory / Map Foundation V0.1.

	Match state only. Map geometry lives in MapConfiguration.
	Does not own political hierarchy; listens to hierarchy ruler lifecycle.
]]

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local MapCatalog = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.MapCatalog)
local MapAdjacency = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.MapAdjacency)
local Hierarchy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.PoliticalHierarchyService)

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local Neutral = require(script.Parent.NeutralDefenseService)
local TerritoryMapService = {}
local revisionByTerritory: { [string]: number } = {}

local ownerChangedBindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
TerritoryMapService.OwnershipChanged = ownerChangedBindable.Event

local started = false
local mapDef
local ownerByTerritory: { [string]: string } = {}
local territoriesByRuler: { [string]: { string } } = {}
local colorIndexByRuler: { [string]: number } = {}
local nextColorIndex = 1

local remotesFolder: Folder
local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?

local function definitionById()
	local byId = {}
	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		byId[t.id] = t
	end
	return byId
end

local function unclaimedIds(): { string }
	local list = {}
	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		if ownerByTerritory[t.id] == nil then
			table.insert(list, t.id)
		end
	end
	table.sort(list)
	return list
end

local function ensureColor(rulerId: string)
	if colorIndexByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
		colorIndexByRuler[rulerId] = nextColorIndex
		nextColorIndex += 1
	end
end

local function buildSnapshot()
	local territories = {}
	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		local owner = ownerByTerritory[t.id]
		table.insert(territories, {
			territoryId = t.id,
			ownerRulerId = owner or "",
			hasOwner = owner ~= nil,
			neutralDefense = if not owner then Neutral.Get(t.id) else nil,
		})
	end
	table.sort(territories, function(a, b)
		return a.territoryId < b.territoryId
	end)
	local rulerColors = {}
	for rulerId, index in colorIndexByRuler do
		table.insert(rulerColors, {
			rulerId = rulerId,
			colorIndex = index,
		})
	end
	table.sort(rulerColors, function(a, b)
		return a.rulerId < b.rulerId
	end)
	return {
		mapId = mapDef.mapId,
		territories = territories,
		rulerColors = rulerColors,
	}
end

local function pushState(toPlayer: Player?)
	local payload = buildSnapshot()
	local encoded = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
	if debugSnapshot then
		debugSnapshot.Value = encoded
	end
	if toPlayer then
		print("[Territory] push State to", toPlayer.Name)
		serverPush:FireClient(toPlayer, "State", encoded)
	else
		print("[Territory] push State all clients")
		serverPush:FireAllClients("State", encoded)
	end
	ownerChangedBindable:Fire()
end

local function unclaimRuler(rulerId: string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return end
	local owned = territoriesByRuler[rulerId]
	if not owned then
		colorIndexByRuler[rulerId] = nil
		return
	end
	for _, territoryId in owned do
		if ownerByTerritory[territoryId] == rulerId then
			revisionByTerritory[territoryId] = (revisionByTerritory[territoryId] or 0) + 1
			ownerByTerritory[territoryId] = nil
			print("[Territory] unclaimed", territoryId, "from", rulerId)
		end
	end
	territoriesByRuler[rulerId] = nil
	colorIndexByRuler[rulerId] = nil
	pushState()
end

local function assignStartingTerritory(rulerId: string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return end
	local existing = territoriesByRuler[rulerId]
	if existing and #existing > 0 then
		return
	end
	local free = unclaimedIds()
	if #free == 0 then
		warn("[Territory] no free starting territory for", rulerId)
		return
	end
	local pick = free[math.random(1, #free)]
	ensureColor(rulerId)
	revisionByTerritory[pick] = (revisionByTerritory[pick] or 0) + 1
	ownerByTerritory[pick] = rulerId
	territoriesByRuler[rulerId] = { pick }
	print("[Territory] assigned", pick, "to", rulerId)
	pushState()
end

function TerritoryMapService.GetOwnershipRevision(territoryId: string): number
	return revisionByTerritory[territoryId] or 0
end

function TerritoryMapService.RefreshPublicState()
	pushState()
end

function TerritoryMapService.GetSnapshot()
	return buildSnapshot()
end

function TerritoryMapService.GetMapId(): string
	return mapDef.mapId
end

function TerritoryMapService.GetMapDefinition()
	return mapDef
end

function TerritoryMapService.CountOwnedByRulers(rulerIds: { string }): number
	local want = {}
	for _, id in rulerIds do
		want[id] = true
	end
	local n = 0
	for _, owner in ownerByTerritory do
		if want[owner] then
			n += 1
		end
	end
	return n
end

function TerritoryMapService.GetOwnerRulerId(territoryId: string): string?
	return ownerByTerritory[territoryId]
end

function TerritoryMapService.GetOwnedTerritoryIds(rulerId: string): { string }
	local owned = territoriesByRuler[rulerId]
	if not owned then
		return {}
	end
	local copy = table.clone(owned)
	table.sort(copy)
	return copy
end

function TerritoryMapService.AreAdjacent(territoryA: string, territoryB: string): boolean
	if territoryA == territoryB then
		return false
	end
	local byId = definitionById()
	local def = byId[territoryA]
	if not def then
		return false
	end
	for _, adj in def.adjacentIds do
		if adj == territoryB then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

-- Authoritative ownership change for conquest / debug. Not a client remote.
function TerritoryMapService.SetOwner(territoryId: string, rulerId: string?): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local byId = definitionById()
	if not byId[territoryId] then
		return false, "Unknown territory."
	end
	revisionByTerritory[territoryId] = (revisionByTerritory[territoryId] or 0) + 1
	local previous = ownerByTerritory[territoryId]
	if previous then
		local list = territoriesByRuler[previous]
		if list then
			local nextList = {}
			for _, id in list do
				if id ~= territoryId then
					table.insert(nextList, id)
				end
			end
			territoriesByRuler[previous] = nextList
		end
	end
	if rulerId == nil or rulerId == "" then
		ownerByTerritory[territoryId] = nil
	else
		ensureColor(rulerId)
		ownerByTerritory[territoryId] = rulerId
		local list = territoriesByRuler[rulerId] or {}
		local already = false
		for _, id in list do
			if id == territoryId then
				already = true
				break
			end
		end
		if not already then
			table.insert(list, territoryId)
		end
		territoriesByRuler[rulerId] = list
	end
	pushState()
	require(script.Parent.VictoryService).Evaluate()
	return true, "Ownership updated."
end

-- Prototype/debug only. Not a client remote.
function TerritoryMapService.DebugSetOwner(territoryId: string, rulerId: string?)
	local ok, err = TerritoryMapService.SetOwner(territoryId, rulerId)
	assert(ok, err)
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any)
	if action == "Ready" then
		print("[Territory] Ready from", player.Name)
		pushState(player)
	end
end

function TerritoryMapService.Start()
	if started then
		return
	end
	started = true

	mapDef = MapCatalog.GetDefault()
	local ok, err = MapAdjacency.Validate(mapDef.territories)
	assert(ok, err)

	remotesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes") :: Folder
	clientIntent = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("TerritoryClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	serverPush = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("TerritoryServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent

	clientIntent.OnServerEvent:Connect(onClientIntent)

	local debugFolder = ServerScriptService:FindFirstChild("TerritoryMapDebug")
	if debugFolder then
		debugFolder:Destroy()
	end
	debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "TerritoryMapDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = ServerScriptService
	debugSnapshot = Instance.new("StringValue")
	debugSnapshot.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	debugSnapshot.Parent = debugFolder
	debugFolder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("SetOwner"):Connect(function()
		local raw = debugFolder:GetAttribute("SetOwner")
		if typeof(raw) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local territoryId, rulerId = string.match(raw, "^(.-)=(.*)$")
		if not territoryId then
			warn("[Territory] debug SetOwner expected territoryId=rulerId")
			return
		end
		if rulerId == nil or rulerId == "" then
			TerritoryMapService.DebugSetOwner(territoryId, nil)
		else
			TerritoryMapService.DebugSetOwner(territoryId, rulerId)
		end
	end)

	Hierarchy.RulerCreated:Connect(function(rulerId: string, userId: number?)
		if typeof(userId) == "number" then
			assignStartingTerritory(rulerId)
		end
	end)
	Hierarchy.RulerRemoving:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		unclaimRuler(rulerId)
	end)

	local snap = Hierarchy.GetSnapshot()
	for _, ruler in snap.rulers do
		if ruler.hasUser == true then
			assignStartingTerritory(ruler.rulerId)
		end
	end

	-- Prototype/debug: running instance for Command Bar / MCP. Not a client remote.
	_G.__TerritoryMapV01 = TerritoryMapService
end

return TerritoryMapService

--[[
	Title Progression V0.1.

	Reads hierarchy + territory ownership. Does not own those systems.
	Prestige is temporary server-side test Prestige (not spent, not an economy).
]]

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local RankRules = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.PoliticalRankRules)
local TitleRequirements = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.TitleRequirements)
local Hierarchy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.PoliticalHierarchyService)
local Territory = require(ServerScriptService.Services.TerritoryMapService)

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local TitleProgressionService = {}

local DEFAULT_PRESTIGE = 0

local started = false
local prestigeByRuler: { [string]: number } = {}
local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?

local function findRuler(rulerId: string): any?
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.rulerId == rulerId then
			return ruler
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function realmControl(rulerId: string): number
	local ruler = findRuler(rulerId)
	if not ruler then
		return 0
	end
	local ids = { ruler.rulerId }
	if typeof(ruler.descendantIds) == "table" then
		for _, id in ruler.descendantIds do
			table.insert(ids, id)
		end
	end
	return Territory.CountOwnedByRulers(ids)
end

local function getPrestige(rulerId: string): number
	local value = prestigeByRuler[rulerId]
	if typeof(value) == "number" then
		return value
	end
	return DEFAULT_PRESTIGE
end

local function buildRow(ruler: any): any
	local currentRank = ruler.titleRank
	local nextRank = RankRules.NextRank(currentRank)
	local mapDef = Territory.GetMapDefinition()
	local control = realmControl(ruler.rulerId)
	local prestige = getPrestige(ruler.rulerId)
	if nextRank == nil then
		return {
			rulerId = ruler.rulerId,
			userId = ruler.userId or 0,
			currentRank = currentRank,
			currentTitleName = RankRules.Name(currentRank),
			hasNext = false,
			nextRank = 0,
			nextTitleName = "",
			establishLabel = "",
			realmControl = control,
			realmRequired = 0,
			prestige = prestige,
			prestigeRequired = 0,
			eligible = false,
			statusText = "Maximum title",
		}
	end
	local req = TitleRequirements.ForRank(mapDef, nextRank)
	if not req then
		return {
			rulerId = ruler.rulerId,
			userId = ruler.userId or 0,
			currentRank = currentRank,
			currentTitleName = RankRules.Name(currentRank),
			hasNext = true,
			nextRank = nextRank,
			nextTitleName = RankRules.Name(nextRank),
			establishLabel = "",
			realmControl = control,
			realmRequired = 0,
			prestige = prestige,
			prestigeRequired = 0,
			eligible = false,
			statusText = "No requirement configured",
		}
	end
	local eligible = control >= req.territoryCount and prestige >= req.prestige
	return {
		rulerId = ruler.rulerId,
		userId = ruler.userId or 0,
		currentRank = currentRank,
		currentTitleName = RankRules.Name(currentRank),
		hasNext = true,
		nextRank = nextRank,
		nextTitleName = req.titleName,
		establishLabel = req.establishLabel,
		realmControl = control,
		realmRequired = req.territoryCount,
		prestige = prestige,
		prestigeRequired = req.prestige,
		eligible = eligible,
		statusText = if eligible then "Eligible" else "Not eligible",
	}
end

local function buildSnapshot()
	local byRulerId = {}
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		table.insert(byRulerId, buildRow(ruler))
	end
	table.sort(byRulerId, function(a, b)
		return a.rulerId < b.rulerId
	end)
	return {
		mapId = Territory.GetMapId(),
		rows = byRulerId,
	}
end

local function pushState(toPlayer: Player?)
	local encoded = HttpService:JSONEncode(buildSnapshot())
	if debugSnapshot then
		debugSnapshot.Value = encoded
	end
	if toPlayer then
		print("[Title] push State to", toPlayer.Name)
		serverPush:FireClient(toPlayer, "State", encoded)
	else
		print("[Title] push State all clients")
		serverPush:FireAllClients("State", encoded)
	end
end

local function notice(player: Player, message: string)
	print("[Title] notice ->", player.Name, message)
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "Notice", message)
end

local function refresh()
	if not started then
		return
	end
	pushState()
end

function TitleProgressionService.GetSnapshot()
	return buildSnapshot()
end

-- Prototype/debug only. Not a client remote.
function TitleProgressionService.DebugSetPrestige(rulerId: string, value: number)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return end
	assert(typeof(value) == "number", "Prestige must be a number")
	assert(findRuler(rulerId), "Missing ruler")
	prestigeByRuler[rulerId] = value
	print("[Title] debug prestige", rulerId, value)
	refresh()
end

function TitleProgressionService.EstablishForPlayer(player: Player): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local rulerId = Hierarchy.GetRulerIdForUserId(player.UserId)
	if not rulerId then
		return false, "No ruler record."
	end
	local ruler = findRuler(rulerId)
	if not ruler then
		return false, "Ruler no longer exists."
	end
	local nextRank = RankRules.NextRank(ruler.titleRank)
	if nextRank == nil then
		return false, "No higher landed title."
	end
	local req = TitleRequirements.ForRank(Territory.GetMapDefinition(), nextRank)
	if not req then
		return false, "No requirement configured."
	end
	local control = realmControl(rulerId)
	local prestige = getPrestige(rulerId)
	if control < req.territoryCount then
		return false, "Insufficient realm control."
	end
	if prestige < req.prestige then
		return false, "Insufficient Prestige."
	end
	local ok, err = Hierarchy.SetTitleRank(rulerId, nextRank)
	if not ok then
		return false, err
	end
	-- Prestige is not spent.
	print("[Title] established", req.titleName, "for", ruler.displayName, "prestige remains", prestige)
	return true, req.establishedNotice
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any)
	if typeof(action) ~= "string" then
		notice(player, "Invalid request.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Ready" then
		print("[Title] Ready from", player.Name)
		pushState(player)
		return
	end
	if action == "Establish" then
		local ok, result = TitleProgressionService.EstablishForPlayer(player)
		notice(player, result)
		if ok then
			refresh()
		end
		return
	end
	notice(player, "Unknown action.")
end

function TitleProgressionService.Start()
	if started then
		return
	end
	started = true

	local remotesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes") :: Folder
	clientIntent = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("TitleClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	serverPush = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("TitleServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	clientIntent.OnServerEvent:Connect(onClientIntent)

	local debugFolder = ServerScriptService:FindFirstChild("TitleProgressionDebug")
	if debugFolder then
		debugFolder:Destroy()
	end
	debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "TitleProgressionDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = ServerScriptService
	local help = Instance.new("StringValue")
	help.Name = "Help"
	help.Value =
		"Prototype only. Play Server Command Bar: _G.__TitleProgressionV01.DebugSetPrestige(rulerId, n). MCP: SetAttribute SetPrestige to rulerId=number."
	help.Parent = debugFolder
	debugSnapshot = Instance.new("StringValue")
	debugSnapshot.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	debugSnapshot.Parent = debugFolder
	debugFolder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("SetPrestige"):Connect(function()
		local raw = debugFolder:GetAttribute("SetPrestige")
		if typeof(raw) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local rulerId, valueStr = string.match(raw, "^(.-)=([%-%d%.]+)$")
		local value = tonumber(valueStr)
		if rulerId and value then
			TitleProgressionService.DebugSetPrestige(rulerId, value)
		else
			warn("[Title] debug SetPrestige expected rulerId=number")
		end
	end)
	debugFolder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("EstablishForUserId"):Connect(function()
		local userId = tonumber(debugFolder:GetAttribute("EstablishForUserId"))
		if not userId then
			return
		end
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		if not player then
			warn("[Title] debug EstablishForUserId: no player")
			return
		end
		local ok, result = TitleProgressionService.EstablishForPlayer(player)
		print("[Title] debug establish", ok, result)
		notice(player, result)
		if ok then
			refresh()
		end
	end)

	Hierarchy.StateChanged:Connect(refresh)
	Territory.OwnershipChanged:Connect(refresh)
	Hierarchy.RulerCreated:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		if prestigeByRuler[rulerId] == nil then
			prestigeByRuler[rulerId] = DEFAULT_PRESTIGE
		end
		refresh()
	end)
	Hierarchy.RulerRemoving:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		prestigeByRuler[rulerId] = nil
		refresh()
	end)

		task.defer(function()
			if player.Parent then
				pushState(player)
			end
		end)
	end)

	refresh()

	-- Prototype/debug: bind the running instance for Command Bar / MCP.
	-- Not a client remote. Do not use from ordinary gameplay.
	_G.__TitleProgressionV01 = TitleProgressionService
end

return TitleProgressionService

assert(game:GetService("ServerScriptService"):WaitForChild("Services"):WaitForChild("VictoryService").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Hierarchy = require(script.Parent.PoliticalHierarchyService)
local Territory = require(script.Parent.TerritoryMapService)
local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local Domination = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.Domination)
local Service = {}
local started = false
local startedAt = 0
local push: RemoteEvent

function Service.GetSnapshot(): any
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return MatchState.GetFinal() end
	local result = Domination.Evaluate(Territory.GetMapDefinition(), Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers, Territory.GetSnapshot().territories)
	return { ended = false, type = "Domination", total = result.total, required = result.required, threshold = result.threshold, counts = result.counts }
end

local function send(player: Player?)
	local payload = HttpService:JSONEncode(Service.GetSnapshot())
	if player then push:FireClient(player, "State", payload) else push:FireAllClients("State", payload) end
end

function Service.Evaluate(): any
	if not started or MatchState.IsEnded() then return MatchState.GetFinal() end
	local rulers = Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers
	local result = Domination.Evaluate(Territory.GetMapDefinition(), rulers, Territory.GetSnapshot().territories)
	if result.winner then
		local root, members = nil, {}
		for _, ruler in rulers do
			if ruler.rulerId == result.winner.rootId then root = ruler end
			if ruler.rootRulerId == result.winner.rootId then
				table.insert(members, { rulerId = ruler.rulerId, userId = ruler.userId, displayName = ruler.displayName, titleName = ruler.titleName })
			end
		end
		assert(root)
		local final = { ended = true, type = "Domination", winnerRootId = root.rulerId, winnerUserId = root.userId,
			winnerName = root.displayName, winnerTitle = root.titleName, realmName = root.displayName .. "'s realm",
			controlled = result.winner.controlled, total = result.total, required = result.required, threshold = result.threshold,
			controlPercent = result.winner.controlled / result.total, members = members, endedAt = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
			durationSeconds = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - startedAt }
		if MatchState.End(final) then
			require(script.Parent.MilitaryService).FreezeMatch()
			require(script.Parent.WarfareService).FreezeMatch()
			print("[Victory] Domination", final.winnerRootId, final.controlled, final.total)
		end
	end
	send(nil)
	return MatchState.GetFinal()
end

function Service.Start()
	if started then return end
	startedAt = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	push = remotes:WaitForChild("VictoryServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local intent = remotes:WaitForChild("VictoryClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	Domination.Evaluate(Territory.GetMapDefinition(), Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers, Territory.GetSnapshot().territories)
	started = true
	intent.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, action) if action == "Ready" then send(player) end end)
	Hierarchy.StateChanged:Connect(Service.Evaluate)
	Territory.OwnershipChanged:Connect(Service.Evaluate)
	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player) task.defer(function() if player.Parent then send(player) end end) end)
	Service.Evaluate()
end

return Service

assert(game:GetService("ServerScriptService"):WaitForChild("Services"):WaitForChild("WarfareService").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

--[[
	Warfare / Conquest V0.1.

	Server-authoritative external wars between independent roots.
	One enemy-controlled territory per war. Military / Armies V0.1 owns
	Troops and battle; this service owns lifecycle, contest/cooldown, and
	Attacker/Defender outcome application. Privileged DebugResolveWar remains.
]]

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService")

local Hierarchy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.PoliticalHierarchyService)
local Territory = require(ServerScriptService.Services.TerritoryMapService)
local Diplomacy = require(ServerScriptService.Services.DiplomacyService)
local WarfareAdjacency = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.WarfareAdjacency)

local MatchState = require(script.Parent.MatchState)
local Neutral = require(script.Parent.NeutralDefenseService)
local WarfareService = {}

type War = {
	warId: string,
	attackerRootId: string,
	defenderRootId: string,
	targetTerritoryId: string,
	status: string,
	targetKind: string,
	targetRevision: number,
	neutralDefenders: number?,
}

local started = false
local warsById: { [string]: War } = {}
local cooldownUntil: { [string]: number } = {}
local clientIntent: RemoteEvent
local serverPush: RemoteEvent
local debugSnapshot: StringValue?
local TERRITORY_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 10

local function militaryModule()
	return require(ServerScriptService.Services.MilitaryService)
end

local function serverNow(): number
	return workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end

local function pruneCooldown(territoryId: string)
	local untilAt = cooldownUntil[territoryId]
	if untilAt ~= nil and serverNow() >= untilAt then
		cooldownUntil[territoryId] = nil
	end
end

local function isTerritoryOnCooldown(territoryId: string): boolean
	pruneCooldown(territoryId)
	return cooldownUntil[territoryId] ~= nil
end

local function beginTerritoryCooldown(territoryId: string)
	cooldownUntil[territoryId] = serverNow() + TERRITORY_COOLDOWN_SECONDS
end

local function activeWarOnTerritory(territoryId: string): War?
	for _, war in warsById do
		if war.status == "Active" and war.targetTerritoryId == territoryId then
			return war
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function findRuler(rulerId: string): any?
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.rulerId == rulerId then
			return ruler
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function isIndependentRoot(ruler: any?): boolean
	return ruler ~= nil and ruler.independent == true
end

local function greaterRealmRulerIds(rootId: string): { string }
	local root = findRuler(rootId)
	if not isIndependentRoot(root) then
		return {}
	end
	local byId: { [string]: any } = {}
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if typeof(ruler.rulerId) == "string" then
			byId[ruler.rulerId] = ruler
		end
	end
	local ids = { rootId }
	local seen: { [string]: boolean } = { [rootId] = true }
	local queue = { rootId }
	local i = 1
	while i <= #queue do
		local current = byId[queue[i]]
		i += 1
		if current and typeof(current.vassalIds) == "table" then
			for _, vassalId in current.vassalIds do
				if typeof(vassalId) == "string" and seen[vassalId] ~= true then
					seen[vassalId] = true
					table.insert(ids, vassalId)
					table.insert(queue, vassalId)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if typeof(root.descendantIds) == "table" then
		for _, id in root.descendantIds do
			if typeof(id) == "string" and seen[id] ~= true then
				seen[id] = true
				table.insert(ids, id)
			end
		end
	end
	return ids
end

local function ownerRootId(territoryId: string): string?
	local ownerId = Territory.GetOwnerRulerId(territoryId)
	if not ownerId then
		return nil
	end
	local owner = findRuler(ownerId)
	if not owner then
		return nil
	end
	if typeof(owner.rootRulerId) == "string" and owner.rootRulerId ~= "" then
		return owner.rootRulerId
	end
	return ownerId
end

local function isOwnedByRealm(territoryId: string, rootId: string): boolean
	local ownerId = Territory.GetOwnerRulerId(territoryId)
	if not ownerId then
		return false
	end
	for _, memberId in greaterRealmRulerIds(rootId) do
		if memberId == ownerId then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function ownedByGreaterRealm(rootId: string): { string }
	local owned = {}
	for _, memberId in greaterRealmRulerIds(rootId) do
		for _, territoryId in Territory.GetOwnedTerritoryIds(memberId) do
			table.insert(owned, territoryId)
		end
	end
	return owned
end

local function isGreaterRealmAdjacent(attackerRootId: string, targetTerritoryId: string): boolean
	local mapDef = Territory.GetMapDefinition()
	return WarfareAdjacency.IsTargetAdjacentToOwned(mapDef.territories, targetTerritoryId, ownedByGreaterRealm(attackerRootId))
end

local function noticePlayer(player: Player, message: string)
	print("[Warfare] notice ->", player.Name, message)
	serverPush:FireClient(player, "Notice", message)
end

local function noticeRulerId(rulerId: string, message: string)
	local ruler = findRuler(rulerId)
	local userId = ruler and tonumber(ruler.userId)
	if typeof(userId) == "number" and userId ~= 0 then
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
		if player then
			noticePlayer(player, message)
		end
	end
end

local function buildSnapshot()
	local wars = {}
	for _, war in warsById do
		if war.status == "Active" then
			table.insert(wars, {
				warId = war.warId,
				attackerRootId = war.attackerRootId,
				defenderRootId = war.defenderRootId,
				targetTerritoryId = war.targetTerritoryId,
				status = war.status,
				targetKind = war.targetKind,
				neutralDefenders = war.neutralDefenders,
			})
		end
	end
	table.sort(wars, function(a, b)
		return a.warId < b.warId
	end)
	local cooldowns = {}
	for territoryId, untilAt in cooldownUntil do
		pruneCooldown(territoryId)
		local remaining = cooldownUntil[territoryId]
		if remaining ~= nil then
			table.insert(cooldowns, {
				territoryId = territoryId,
				endsAt = remaining,
			})
		end
	end
	table.sort(cooldowns, function(a, b)
		return a.territoryId < b.territoryId
	end)
	return { wars = wars, cooldowns = cooldowns }
end

local function pushState(toPlayer: Player?)
	local payload = buildSnapshot()
	local encoded = HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
	if debugSnapshot then
		debugSnapshot.Value = encoded
	end
	if toPlayer then
		print("[Warfare] push State to", toPlayer.Name)
		serverPush:FireClient(toPlayer, "State", encoded)
	else
		print("[Warfare] push State all clients wars=", #payload.wars)
		serverPush:FireAllClients("State", encoded)
	end
end

local function endWar(war: War, reason: string)
	if war.status ~= "Active" then
		return
	end
	war.status = "Resolved"
	warsById[war.warId] = nil
	beginTerritoryCooldown(war.targetTerritoryId)
	print("[Warfare] ended", war.warId, reason)
end

local function targetStillOriginalDefender(war: War): boolean
	if war.targetKind == "Unclaimed" then
		return Territory.GetOwnerRulerId(war.targetTerritoryId) == nil and Territory.GetOwnershipRevision(war.targetTerritoryId) == war.targetRevision
	end
	local currentRoot = ownerRootId(war.targetTerritoryId)
	return currentRoot == war.defenderRootId
end

function WarfareService.GetSnapshot()
	return buildSnapshot()
end

function WarfareService.DeclareWar(attackerRootId: string, targetTerritoryId: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	if typeof(targetTerritoryId) ~= "string" or targetTerritoryId == "" then
		return false, "Cannot declare war: invalid target."
	end
	local attacker = findRuler(attackerRootId)
	if not isIndependentRoot(attacker) then
		return false, "Subjects cannot independently declare external war."
	end
	local mapDef = Territory.GetMapDefinition()
	local exists = false
	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		if t.id == targetTerritoryId and t.conquerable ~= false then
			exists = true
			break
		end
	end
	if not exists then
		return false, "Cannot declare war: unknown territory."
	end
	local ownerId = Territory.GetOwnerRulerId(targetTerritoryId)
	local neutral = if not ownerId then Neutral.Get(targetTerritoryId) else nil
	if isOwnedByRealm(targetTerritoryId, attackerRootId) then
		return false, "Cannot declare war: target belongs to your realm."
	end
	local defenderRoot = ownerRootId(targetTerritoryId) or ""
	local defender = findRuler(defenderRoot)
	if not neutral and not isIndependentRoot(defender) then
		return false, "Cannot declare war: defender is not an independent realm."
	end
	if not isGreaterRealmAdjacent(attackerRootId, targetTerritoryId) then
		return false, "Cannot declare war: target not adjacent."
	end
	if not neutral and Diplomacy.HasNonAggressionPact(attackerRootId, defenderRoot) then
		return false, "Cannot declare war: Non-Aggression Pact active."
	end
	if activeWarOnTerritory(targetTerritoryId) then
		return false, "Territory is already contested."
	end
	if isTerritoryOnCooldown(targetTerritoryId) then
		return false, "Territory is on post-war cooldown."
	end
	for _, war in warsById do
		if war.status == "Active" and war.attackerRootId == attackerRootId and war.targetTerritoryId == targetTerritoryId then
			return false, "Cannot declare war: a matching war is already active."
		end
	end
	local warId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	local created: War = {
		warId = warId,
		attackerRootId = attackerRootId,
		defenderRootId = defenderRoot,
		targetTerritoryId = targetTerritoryId,
		status = "Active",
		targetKind = if neutral then "Unclaimed" else "Player",
		targetRevision = Territory.GetOwnershipRevision(targetTerritoryId),
		neutralDefenders = if neutral then neutral.current else nil,
	}
	warsById[warId] = created
	militaryModule().OnWarCreated(created)
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(attackerRootId, if neutral then "Conquest begun. Commit Troops in Military." else "War declared.")
	noticeRulerId(defenderRoot, "War declared against your realm.")
	return true, warId
end

function WarfareService.CancelWar(warId: string, reason: string)
	local war = warsById[warId]
	if not war or war.status ~= "Active" then
		return
	end
	local attacker = war.attackerRootId
	local defender = war.defenderRootId
	endWar(war, reason)
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(attacker, "War cancelled (" .. reason .. ").")
	noticeRulerId(defender, "War cancelled (" .. reason .. ").")
end

function WarfareService.ApplyMilitaryOutcome(warId: string, outcome: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local war = warsById[warId]
	if not war or war.status ~= "Active" then
		return false, "War is no longer active."
	end
	if outcome ~= "Attacker" and outcome ~= "Defender" then
		return false, "Unknown resolution."
	end
	if war.targetKind == "Unclaimed" and (not targetStillOriginalDefender(war) or not isIndependentRoot(findRuler(war.attackerRootId))) then
		endWar(war, "stale neutral conquest")
		pushState()
		return false, "Conquest stale; no transfer."
	end
	if outcome == "Defender" then
		local attacker = war.attackerRootId
		local defender = war.defenderRootId
		endWar(war, "defender victory")
		pushState()
		noticeRulerId(attacker, "War ended: defender victory. Ownership unchanged.")
		noticeRulerId(defender, "War ended: defender victory. Ownership unchanged.")
		return true, "Defender victory."
	end
	local attackerId = war.attackerRootId
	local defenderId = war.defenderRootId
	local targetId = war.targetTerritoryId
	if not targetStillOriginalDefender(war) then
		endWar(war, "attacker victory stale target")
		pushState()
		noticeRulerId(attackerId, "War ended: attacker victory. Target no longer belongs to the original defender.")
		noticeRulerId(defenderId, "War ended: attacker victory. Target no longer belongs to the original defender.")
		return true, "Attacker victory recorded; territory was not transferred."
	end
	endWar(war, "attacker victory")
	local ok, err = Territory.SetOwner(targetId, attackerId)
	if not ok then
		pushState()
		return false, err
	end
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(attackerId, "War ended: attacker victory. You now hold " .. targetId .. ".")
	noticeRulerId(defenderId, "War ended: attacker victory. " .. targetId .. " was lost.")
	return true, "Attacker victory."
end

function WarfareService.IsTargetCurrent(warId: string): boolean
	local war = warsById[warId]
	return war ~= nil and targetStillOriginalDefender(war)
end

function WarfareService.FreezeMatch()
	local snapshot = {}
	for _, war in warsById do table.insert(snapshot, war) end
	for _, war in snapshot do endWar(war, "match ended") end
	pushState()
end

function WarfareService.GetContestInspect()
	return buildSnapshot()
end

function WarfareService.DebugResolveWar(warId: string, outcome: string): (boolean, string)
	if MatchState.IsEnded() then return false, "Match ended." end
	local war = warsById[warId]
	if not war or war.status ~= "Active" then
		return false, "War is no longer active."
	end
	if outcome ~= "Attacker" and outcome ~= "Defender" then
		return false, "Unknown resolution."
	end
	local Military = militaryModule()
	if Military.IsMechanicallyResolved(warId) then
		return false, "War is already resolving."
	end
	Military.UnwindPreResolution(warId)
	if outcome == "Defender" then
		local attacker = war.attackerRootId
		local defender = war.defenderRootId
		endWar(war, "defender victory")
		pushState()
		noticeRulerId(attacker, "War ended: defender victory. Ownership unchanged.")
		noticeRulerId(defender, "War ended: defender victory. Ownership unchanged.")
		return true, "Defender victory."
	end
	local attacker = findRuler(war.attackerRootId)
	if not isIndependentRoot(attacker) then
		endWar(war, "attacker no longer independent")
		pushState()
		return false, "War terminated: attacker is no longer independent."
	end
	if not targetStillOriginalDefender(war) then
		endWar(war, "stale target")
		pushState()
		noticeRulerId(war.attackerRootId, "War ended: target no longer belongs to the original defender.")
		noticeRulerId(war.defenderRootId, "War ended: target no longer belongs to the original defender.")
		return false, "War is stale. Territory was not transferred."
	end
	local attackerId = war.attackerRootId
	local defenderId = war.defenderRootId
	local targetId = war.targetTerritoryId
	endWar(war, "attacker victory")
	local ok, err = Territory.SetOwner(targetId, attackerId)
	if not ok then
		pushState()
		return false, err
	end
	pushState()
	noticeRulerId(attackerId, "War ended: attacker victory. You now hold " .. targetId .. ".")
	noticeRulerId(defenderId, "War ended: attacker victory. " .. targetId .. " was lost.")
	return true, "Attacker victory."
end

local function cancelInvolving(rulerId: string, reason: string)
	local removed = false
	local toEnd = {}
	for _, war in warsById do
		if war.status == "Active" and (war.attackerRootId == rulerId or war.defenderRootId == rulerId) then
			table.insert(toEnd, war)
		end
	end
	local Military = militaryModule()
	for _, war in toEnd do
		if Military.IsMechanicallyResolved(war.warId) then
			-- Mechanical result stays authoritative through presentation.
		else
			Military.UnwindPreResolution(war.warId)
			local other = if war.attackerRootId == rulerId then war.defenderRootId else war.attackerRootId
			endWar(war, reason)
			noticeRulerId(other, "War cancelled (" .. reason .. ").")
			removed = true
		end
	end
	if removed then
		noticeRulerId(rulerId, "External wars ended (" .. reason .. ").")
		pushState()
	end
end

local function invalidateStaleWars()
	local removed = false
	local Military = militaryModule()
	for _, war in warsById do
		if war.status == "Active" and not targetStillOriginalDefender(war) then
			if Military.IsMechanicallyResolved(war.warId) then
				-- Do not reopen a mechanically final battle.
			else
				Military.UnwindPreResolution(war.warId)
				endWar(war, "stale after ownership change")
				noticeRulerId(war.attackerRootId, "War ended: target no longer belongs to the original defender.")
				noticeRulerId(war.defenderRootId, "War ended: target no longer belongs to the original defender.")
				removed = true
			end
		end
	end
	if removed then
		pushState()
	end
end

local function syncIndependence()
	for _, ruler in Hierarchy.GetSnapshot().rulers do
		if ruler.independent ~= true then
			cancelInvolving(ruler.rulerId, "no longer independent")
		end
	end
end

local function onClientIntent(player: Player, action: any, a: any)
	if typeof(action) ~= "string" then
		noticePlayer(player, "Invalid request.")
		return
	end
	if action == "Ready" then
		print("[Warfare] Ready from", player.Name)
		pushState(player)
		return
	end
	local fromId = Hierarchy.GetRulerIdForUserId(player.UserId)
	if not fromId then
		noticePlayer(player, "No ruler record.")
		return
	end
	if action == "DeclareWar" then
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" then
			noticePlayer(player, "Cannot declare war: invalid target.")
			return
		end
		local ok, result = WarfareService.DeclareWar(fromId, a)
		if not ok then
			noticePlayer(player, result)
		end
		return
	end
	noticePlayer(player, "Unknown action.")
end

function WarfareService.Start()
	if started then
		return
	end
	started = true

	local remotesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes") :: Folder
	clientIntent = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("WarfareClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	serverPush = remotesFolder:WaitForChild("WarfareServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	clientIntent.OnServerEvent:Connect(onClientIntent)

	local debugFolder = ServerScriptService:FindFirstChild("WarfareDebug")
	if debugFolder then
		debugFolder:Destroy()
	end
	debugFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	debugFolder.Name = "WarfareDebug"
	debugFolder.Parent = ServerScriptService
	debugSnapshot = Instance.new("StringValue")
	debugSnapshot.Name = "LastSnapshot"
	debugSnapshot.Parent = debugFolder
	local help = Instance.new("StringValue")
	help.Name = "Help"
	help.Value =
		"Prototype only. Invoke('Resolve', warId, 'Attacker'|'Defender'). Directors: ServerScriptService.WarfareDebug.Invoke"
	help.Parent = debugFolder
	local invoke = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
	invoke.Name = "Invoke"
	invoke.Parent = debugFolder
	invoke.OnInvoke = function(action: any, a: any, b: any)
		if action == "Declare" then
			local ok, result = WarfareService.DeclareWar(a, b)
			print("[Warfare] debug Invoke Declare", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Resolve" then
			local ok, result = WarfareService.DebugResolveWar(a, b)
			print("[Warfare] debug Invoke Resolve", ok, result)
			return ok, result
		elseif action == "Contest" then
			local snap = WarfareService.GetContestInspect()
			print("[Warfare] debug Contest", snap)
			return true, snap
		end
		return false, "Unknown debug action."
	end

	_G.__WarfareV01 = WarfareService

	Hierarchy.StateChanged:Connect(syncIndependence)
	Hierarchy.RulerRemoving:Connect(function(rulerId: string)
		cancelInvolving(rulerId, "left the match")
	end)
	Territory.OwnershipChanged:Connect(invalidateStaleWars)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		task.defer(function()
			if player.Parent then
				pushState(player)
			end
		end)
	end)

	pushState()
	print("[Warfare] service started")
end

return WarfareService
]==========],"ServerScriptService/Services/WarfareService.luau");

--[[
	Project-owned admin command feedback. Display only.
	Does not invoke AdminBridge. Does not trust the client for mutations.

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

	InspectTerritory = true,

	InspectGold = "Gold",
	InspectTroops = "Troops",
	InspectMilitary = "Military",
	InspectComms = "Communications",
	InspectContest = "War contest",
	InspectVictory = "Match victory",
	CheckVictory = "Match victory",
	InspectNeutral = "Unclaimed militia",
	SetOwner = "Territory owner",
	SetPrestige = "Prestige",
	SetGold = "Gold",
	AddGold = "Gold",
	SpendGold = "Gold",
	ResolveWar = "War",
}

local function prettyMessage(raw: string): string
	local trimmed = string.gsub(raw, "^%s+", "")
	trimmed = string.gsub(trimmed, "%s+$", "")
	if string.sub(trimmed, 1, 1) == "{" or string.sub(trimmed, 1, 1) == "[" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(trimmed)
		end)
		if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then
			local lines = {}
			local function expand(values: any, depth: number)
				local keys = {}
				for key in values do table.insert(keys, key) end
				table.sort(keys, function(a, b) return tostring(a) < tostring(b) end)
				for _, key in keys do
					local value = values[key]
					local prefix = string.rep("  ", depth) .. tostring(key) .. ": "
					if typeof(value) == "table" and depth < 12 then
						table.insert(lines, prefix)
						expand(value, depth + 1)
					else
						table.insert(lines, prefix .. tostring(value))
					end
				end
			end
			expand(decoded, 0)
			return table.concat(lines, "\n")
		end
	end
	return raw
end

function AdminFeedbackController.Start()
	if started or _G.__AdminFeedbackStarted == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__AdminFeedbackStarted = true

	local gui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 12)
	if not (gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		warn("[Admin] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; feedback idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__AdminFeedbackStarted = nil
		return
	end

	local function ensureInspectPanel(): (Frame, TextLabel, TextLabel, TextButton)
		local existing = gui:FindFirstChild("AdminInspectPanel")
		if existing and existing:IsA("Frame") then
			local header = existing:FindFirstChild("Header")
			local body = existing:FindFirstChild("Body", true)
			local closeBtn = existing:FindFirstChild("CloseButton")
			if header and header:IsA("TextLabel") and body and body:IsA("TextLabel") and closeBtn and closeBtn:IsA("TextButton") then
				return existing, header, body, closeBtn
			end
			existing:Destroy()
		end
		local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
		panel.Name = "AdminInspectPanel"
		panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		panel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 84)
		panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 248)

		panel.Active = true
		panel.ZIndex = 28
		panel.Parent = gui

		header.Name = "Header"
		header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 22)
		header.Text = "Inspect"
		header.Parent = panel
		local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		closeBtn.Name = "CloseButton"
		closeBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
		closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		closeBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 22)
		closeBtn.Text = "Close"
		closeBtn.Parent = panel

		scroll.Name = "BodyScroll"
		scroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 28)
		scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -28)
		scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
		scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 6
		scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		scroll.Parent = panel
		local body = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		body.Name = "Body"
		body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
		body.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		body.TextWrapped = true
		body.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		body.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		body.Text = ""
		body.Parent = scroll
		return panel, header, body, closeBtn
	end

	local inspect, inspectHeader, inspectBody, closeBtn = ensureInspectPanel()
	local resultToast = gui:WaitForChild("ResultToast") :: Frame
	local resultToastLabel = resultToast:WaitForChild("Label") :: TextLabel

	inspectScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
	inspectScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	inspectBody.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	local function fitInspect()
		local width = math.max(1, inspectScroll.AbsoluteSize.X - 8)
		local height = TextService:GetTextSize(inspectBody.Text, inspectBody.TextSize, inspectBody.Font, Vector2.new(width, 100000)).Y
		inspectBody.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, height)
		inspectScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, height + 8)
	end
	inspectBody:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fitInspect)
	inspectScroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fitInspect)
	fitInspect()

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local feedbackWait = remotes:WaitForChild("AdminFeedbackEvent", 20)
	if not (feedbackWait and feedbackWait:IsA("RemoteEvent")) then
		warn("[Admin] AdminFeedbackEvent missing; feedback idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__AdminFeedbackStarted = nil
		return
	end
	local feedback = feedbackWait :: RemoteEvent

	local toastToken = 0

	local function showToast(message: string, kind: string)
		toastToken += 1
		local token = toastToken
		PrototypeGuiTheme.applyToast(resultToast, kind)
		resultToastLabel.Text = message
		resultToast.Visible = true
		task.delay(6, function()
			if token == toastToken then
				resultToast.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end

	closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		inspect.Visible = false
	end)

	feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(payload: any)
		if typeof(payload) ~= "table" then
			return
		end
		local ok = payload.ok == true
		local operation = tostring(payload.operation or "")
		local message = prettyMessage(tostring(payload.message or ""))
		if not ok then
			inspect.Visible = false
			showToast(message, "error")
			return
		end
		if INSPECT[operation] then
			inspectHeader.Text = TITLES[operation] or "Inspect"
			inspectScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
			inspectBody.Text = message
			inspect.Visible = true
		else
			inspect.Visible = false
			showToast(message, "ok")
		end
	end)

	print("[Admin] feedback controller started.")
end

return AdminFeedbackController

assert(game:GetService("StarterPlayer"):WaitForChild("StarterPlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("Controllers"):WaitForChild("CommunicationsController").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.CommunicationsConfig)

local Controller = {}

function Controller.Start()
	if started then return end
	started = true
	local player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local panel = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui"):WaitForChild("CommunicationsPanel") :: ScrollingFrame

			Vector2.new(math.max(1, transcript.AbsoluteSize.X - 10), 100000)).Y + 8
		body.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None

	TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration.Enabled = false
	TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.Enabled = false
	TextChatService.ChannelTabsConfiguration.Enabled = false

	local function refresh()
		local row = rows[selected]
		local canSend = row ~= nil and row.canSend == true and not observing
		input.TextEditable = canSend
		send.Active = canSend and not sending
		Theme.applyPrimaryButton(send, send.Active)
		send.Text = if sending then "…" else "Send"
		status.Text = if observing then "Observer (native receipts only)" elseif row then
			row.label .. (if canSend then "" else " — unavailable") else "History — unavailable"
		body.Text = table.concat(if observing then observerLog else logs[selected] or {}, "\n\n")
		fitTranscript()
		muteButton.Visible = observing
		muteButton.Text = if muted then "Unmute" else "Mute"
		task.defer(function()
			transcript.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, transcript.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - transcript.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y))
		end)
	end
	local function append(id: string, line: string)
		local log = logs[id]
		if not log then log = {}; logs[id] = log end
		table.insert(log, line)
		while #log > Config.HistoryLimit do table.remove(log, 1) end
	end
	local function explain()
		local row = rows[selected]
		if row and not observing then
			append(selected, "[SYSTEM] " .. (Config.Help[row.kind] or "This context is unavailable."))
			shown[selected] = true
		end
		refresh()
	end
	local function selectContext(id: string)
		selected, observing = id, false
		contacts.Visible = false
		transcript.Visible = true
		unread[id] = 0
		if not shown[id] then explain() else refresh() end
		renderList()
	end
	local function button(parent: Instance, text: string, y: number, callback: () -> ())
		local item = Instance.new("TextButton")
		item.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 24)
		item.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y)
		item.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.row
		item.BorderSizePixel = 0
		item.TextColor3 = Theme.Colors.textPrimary
		item.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		item.TextSize = 14
		item.Text = text
		item.Parent = parent
		item.Activated:Connect(callback)
	end
	renderList = function()
		for _, child in list:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end end
		local ids = {}
		for id, row in rows do if not row.observerOnly or logs[id] then table.insert(ids, id) end end
		for id in logs do if not rows[id] then table.insert(ids, id) end end
		table.sort(ids)
		for i, id in ids do
			local row = rows[id]
			local label = if row then row.label else id .. " (history)"
			local count = unread[id] or 0
			button(list, (if id == selected then "› " else "") .. label .. (if count > 0 then " (" .. count .. ")" else ""), (i - 1) * 26,
				function() selectContext(id) end)
		end
		list.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, #ids * 26)
	end
	local function received(id: string)
		local message, meta = pending[id], identity[id]
		if not message or not meta or seen[id] then return end
		seen[id] = true
		if message.TextSource and message.TextSource.UserId == player.UserId and submitted[id] then
			if input.Text == submitted[id] then input.Text = "" end
			submitted[id] = nil
			sending = false
		end
		pending[id], identity[id] = nil, nil
		if meta.ordinary then
			append(meta.contextId, meta.prefix .. message.Text)
			if selected ~= meta.contextId or observing then unread[meta.contextId] = (unread[meta.contextId] or 0) + 1 end
		end
		if meta.observing and not muted then
			table.insert(observerLog, meta.observerPrefix .. message.Text)
			while #observerLog > Config.HistoryLimit do table.remove(observerLog, 1) end
		end
		renderList()
		refresh()
		task.delay(30, function() seen[id] = nil end)
	end
	TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(function(message)
		local channel = message.TextChannel
		if message.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success or not message.TextSource
			or not channel or not channel:GetAttribute("CommunicationsContextId") then return end
		local id = message.MessageId
		pending[id] = message
		received(id)
		task.delay(30, function() pending[id], identity[id] = nil, nil end)
	end)
	push.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(action, data)
		if action == "Identity" and typeof(data) == "table" and typeof(data.messageId) == "string" then
			identity[data.messageId] = data
			received(data.messageId)
			task.delay(30, function() identity[data.messageId] = nil end)
		elseif action == "State" and typeof(data) == "table" then
			rows = {}
			for _, row in data.contexts do rows[row.id] = row end
			availableContacts = data.contacts
			observe.Visible = data.isAdmin
			if not data.isAdmin then observing = false end
			if not shown[selected] and rows[selected] and not rows[selected].observerOnly then explain() end
			renderList()
			refresh()
		elseif action == "Opened" and typeof(data) == "string" then selectContext(data)
		elseif action == "Notice" and typeof(data) == "string" then status.Text = data end
	end)
	local function submit()
		local row = rows[selected]
		local text = string.match(input.Text, "^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
		if observing or sending or not row or not row.canSend or text == "" then return end
		if string.lower(text) == "help" then input.Text = ""; explain(); return end
		local channel = channels:FindFirstChild(row.channelName)
		if not channel or not channel:IsA("TextChannel") then status.Text = "Native channel unavailable."; return end
		sending = true
		refresh()
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, result = pcall(function() return channel:SendAsync(text) end)
			sending = false
			refresh()
			if not ok or not result then
				status.Text = "Message not delivered by Roblox."
			elseif result.Status == Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Sending or result.Status == Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then
				submitted[result.MessageId] = text
				if seen[result.MessageId] then
					if input.Text == text then input.Text = "" end
					submitted[result.MessageId] = nil
				end
				task.delay(15, function()
					if submitted[result.MessageId] then
						submitted[result.MessageId] = nil
						status.Text = "Delivery not confirmed by Roblox."
					end
				end)
			else
				status.Text = "Message blocked by Roblox."
			end
			-- Only native MessageReceived + authoritative label can append player text.
		end)
	end
	send.Activated:Connect(submit)
	input.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then submit() end end)
	help.Activated:Connect(explain)
	direct.Activated:Connect(function()
		contacts.Visible = not contacts.Visible
		transcript.Visible = not contacts.Visible
		for _, child in contacts:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end end
		for i, contact in availableContacts do button(contacts, contact.name, (i - 1) * 26, function() intent:FireServer("OpenDirect", contact.userId) end) end
		contacts.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, #availableContacts * 26)
		if #availableContacts == 0 then status.Text = "No currently neighboring contact." end
	end)
	observe.Activated:Connect(function()
		observing = not observing
		contacts.Visible = false
		transcript.Visible = true
		refresh()
	end)
	muteButton.Activated:Connect(function() muted = not muted; refresh() end)
	intent:FireServer("Ready")
	refresh()
end
return Controller
