--[[
	Prototype diplomacy panel. Sends intents only.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

local PrototypeGuiTheme = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Util"):WaitForChild("PrototypeGuiTheme"))

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local DiplomacyController = {}

local started = false

local function decodeState(payload: any): any?
	if typeof(payload) == "string" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload)
		end)
		if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then
			return decoded
		end
		return nil
	elseif typeof(payload) == "table" then
		return payload
	end
	return nil
end

function DiplomacyController.Start()
	if started or _G.__DiplomacyV01Started == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__DiplomacyV01Started = true

	local gui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 12)
	if not (gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		warn("[Diplomacy] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; panel idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__DiplomacyV01Started = nil
		return
	end

	local panel = gui:WaitForChild("DiplomacyPanel") :: Frame
	local status = panel:WaitForChild("Status") :: TextLabel
	local list = panel:WaitForChild("List") :: ScrollingFrame
	local overlay = gui:WaitForChild("DiplomacyOfferOverlay") :: Frame
	overlay.Visible = false
	local overlayExtra = overlay:WaitForChild("ExtraList") :: Frame
	local overlayBody = overlay:FindFirstChild("Body")
	local overlayButtons = overlay:FindFirstChild("Buttons")
	if overlayBody and overlayBody:IsA("GuiObject") then
		overlayBody.Visible = false
	end
	if overlayButtons and overlayButtons:IsA("GuiObject") then
		overlayButtons.Visible = false
	end
	local rowTemplate = gui:WaitForChild("Templates"):WaitForChild("DiplomacyRow")
	local cardTemplate = gui:WaitForChild("Templates"):WaitForChild("DiplomacyOfferCard")
	local resultToast = gui:WaitForChild("ResultToast") :: Frame
	local resultToastLabel = resultToast:WaitForChild("Label") :: TextLabel

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local intent = remotes:WaitForChild("DiplomacyClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local push = remotes:WaitForChild("DiplomacyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local hierarchyPush = remotes:WaitForChild("HierarchyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent

	local dip = { agreements = {}, requests = {} }
	local hierarchy = { rulers = {} }
	local noticeToken = 0

	local function showNotice(message: string)
		noticeToken += 1
		local token = noticeToken
		resultToastLabel.Text = message
		resultToast.Visible = true
		task.delay(6, function()
			if token == noticeToken then
				resultToast.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end

	local function rulerById(id: string): any?
		for _, ruler in hierarchy.rulers do
			if ruler.rulerId == id then
				return ruler
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function myRuler(): any?
		for _, ruler in hierarchy.rulers do
			if tonumber(ruler.userId) == player.UserId then
				return ruler
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function agreementBetween(a: string, b: string): any?
		local keyA = if a < b then a else b
		local keyB = if a < b then b else a
		for _, row in dip.agreements do
			if row.leftRulerId == keyA and row.rightRulerId == keyB then
				return row
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function pendingBetween(a: string, b: string, kind: string): any?
		for _, request in dip.requests do
			if request.agreementType == kind then
				local same = (request.fromRulerId == a and request.toRulerId == b)
					or (request.fromRulerId == b and request.toRulerId == a)
				if same then
					return request
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function clearFrames(container: Instance)
		for _, child in container:GetChildren() do
			if child:IsA("Frame") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	local function stylePairButtons(offerBtn: TextButton, breakBtn: TextButton, hasAgreement: boolean, canAct: boolean, pending: boolean)
		if hasAgreement then
			offerBtn.Visible = false
			breakBtn.Visible = true
			breakBtn.Active = canAct
			if canAct then
				PrototypeGuiTheme.applyDangerButton(breakBtn)
			else
				PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(breakBtn, false)
			end
		else
			breakBtn.Visible = false
			offerBtn.Visible = true
			local enable = canAct and not pending
			offerBtn.Active = enable
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(offerBtn, enable)
			if pending then
				offerBtn.Text = "Pending..."
			end
		end
		offerBtn.TextSize = 12
		breakBtn.TextSize = 12
	end

	local function refresh()
		local me = myRuler()
		local actorId = if me and me.independent == true then me.rulerId else (if me then me.rootRulerId else nil)
		local canAct = me ~= nil and me.independent == true
		if not me then
			status.Text = "Waiting for server state..."
		elseif canAct then
			status.Text = "Independent realm. Offer or break Alliance / NAP with other independent realms."
		else
			local root = rulerById(me.rootRulerId)
			local rootName = if root then (root.titleName .. " " .. root.displayName) else "your liege"
			status.Text = "Subject of " .. rootName .. ". External diplomacy follows their realm (read-only)."
		end

		clearFrames(list)
		local order = 0
		if actorId then
			for _, ruler in hierarchy.rulers do
				if ruler.independent == true and ruler.rulerId ~= actorId then
					order += 1
					local row = rowTemplate:Clone()
					row.Name = "DipRow_" .. tostring(order)
					row.Visible = true
					row.LayoutOrder = order
					row.Parent = list
					local nameLabel = row:WaitForChild("NameLabel") :: TextLabel
					nameLabel.Text = string.format("%s %s", ruler.titleName, ruler.displayName)
					local rel = agreementBetween(actorId, ruler.rulerId)
					local bits = {}
					if rel and rel.alliance == true then
						table.insert(bits, "Alliance")
					end
					if rel and rel.nap == true then
						table.insert(bits, "NAP")
					end
					local relLabel = row:WaitForChild("RelationLabel") :: TextLabel
					relLabel.Text = if #bits > 0 then table.concat(bits, " · ") else "No agreements"
					local buttonRow = row:WaitForChild("ButtonRow")
					local offerA = buttonRow:WaitForChild("OfferAlliance") :: TextButton
					local breakA = buttonRow:WaitForChild("BreakAlliance") :: TextButton
					local offerN = buttonRow:WaitForChild("OfferNAP") :: TextButton
					local breakN = buttonRow:WaitForChild("BreakNAP") :: TextButton
					offerA.Text = "Offer Alliance"
					breakA.Text = "Break Alliance"
					offerN.Text = "Offer NAP"
					breakN.Text = "Break NAP"
					local pendingA = pendingBetween(actorId, ruler.rulerId, "Alliance") ~= nil
					local pendingN = pendingBetween(actorId, ruler.rulerId, "NAP") ~= nil
					stylePairButtons(offerA, breakA, rel ~= nil and rel.alliance == true, canAct, pendingA)
					stylePairButtons(offerN, breakN, rel ~= nil and rel.nap == true, canAct, pendingN)
					local targetId = ruler.rulerId
					offerA.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						if canAct and offerA.Active then
							intent:FireServer("OfferAlliance", targetId)
						end
					end)
					offerN.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						if canAct and offerN.Active then
							intent:FireServer("OfferNAP", targetId)
						end
					end)
					breakA.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						if canAct and breakA.Active then
							intent:FireServer("BreakAlliance", targetId)
						end
					end)
					breakN.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						if canAct and breakN.Active then
							intent:FireServer("BreakNAP", targetId)
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		if order == 0 then
			status.Text = status.Text .. "\nNo other independent realms."
		end

		clearFrames(overlayExtra)
		local incoming = 0
		if me and me.independent == true then
			for _, request in dip.requests do
				if request.toRulerId == me.rulerId then
					incoming += 1
					local card = cardTemplate:Clone()
					card.Name = "DipOffer_" .. tostring(incoming)
					card.Visible = true
					card.LayoutOrder = incoming
					card.Parent = overlayExtra
					local from = rulerById(request.fromRulerId)
					local fromName = if from then (from.titleName .. " " .. from.displayName) else request.fromRulerId
					local kind = if request.agreementType == "NAP" then "a Non-Aggression Pact" else "an Alliance"
					local label = card:WaitForChild("Label") :: TextLabel
					label.Text = string.format("%s offers %s.", fromName, kind)
					local accept = card:FindFirstChild("AcceptButton", true) :: TextButton
					local decline = card:FindFirstChild("DeclineButton", true) :: TextButton
					accept.Visible = true
					decline.Visible = true
					PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(accept, true)
					PrototypeGuiTheme.applyDangerButton(decline)
					local requestId = request.requestId
					accept.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						intent:FireServer("Accept", requestId)
					end)
					decline.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						intent:FireServer("Decline", requestId)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		overlay.Visible = incoming > 0
	end

	push.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "Notice" and typeof(payload) == "string" then
			showNotice(payload)
			return
		end
		if kind ~= "State" then
			return
		end
		local data = decodeState(payload)
		if not data then
			warn("[Diplomacy] State decode failed")
			return
		end
		dip.agreements = data.agreements or {}
		dip.requests = data.requests or {}
		refresh()
	end)

	hierarchyPush.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind ~= "State" then
			return
		end
		local data = decodeState(payload)
		if data and typeof(data.rulers) == "table" then
			hierarchy.rulers = data.rulers
			refresh()
		end
	end)

	refresh()
	intent:FireServer("Ready")
	print("[Diplomacy] controller started; Ready sent")
end

return DiplomacyController
]==========],"StarterPlayerScripts/Controllers/DiplomacyController.luau");
assert(game:GetService("StarterPlayer"):WaitForChild("StarterPlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("Controllers"):WaitForChild("EconomyController").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

--[[
	Player-relevant Economy V0.1 HUD.
	Ready only. Never sends Gold mutations.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local PrototypeGuiTheme = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Util"):WaitForChild("PrototypeGuiTheme"))

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local EconomyController = {}

local started = false

local function decodeState(payload: any): any?
	if typeof(payload) == "string" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload)
		end)
		if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then
			return decoded
		end
		return nil
	elseif typeof(payload) == "table" then
		return payload
	end
	return nil
end

local function formatCountdown(remaining: number): string
	local secs = math.max(0, math.ceil(remaining))
	return string.format("0:%02d", secs)
end

function EconomyController.Start()
	if started or _G.__EconomyV01Started == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__EconomyV01Started = true

	local gui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 12)
	if not (gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		warn("[Economy] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; HUD idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__EconomyV01Started = nil
		return
	end

	local panel = gui:WaitForChild("EconomyPanel") :: Frame
	local body = panel:WaitForChild("Body") :: TextLabel

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local intent = remotes:WaitForChild("EconomyClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local push = remotes:WaitForChild("EconomyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent

	local gold = 0
	local income = 0
	local nextPayoutAt = 0
	local hasState = false

	local function render(remainingOverride: number?)
		if not hasState then
			body.Text = "Gold: —\nIncome: —\nNext payout: —"
			return
		end
		local remaining = remainingOverride
		if remaining == nil then
			remaining = nextPayoutAt - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
		end
		body.Text = string.format(
			"Gold: %d\nIncome: +%d Gold/min\nNext payout: %s",
			gold,
			income,
			formatCountdown(remaining)
		)
	end

	push.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "State" then
			local state = decodeState(payload)
			if not state then
				return
			end
			gold = tonumber(state.gold) or 0
			income = tonumber(state.incomeGoldPerMinute) or 0
			nextPayoutAt = tonumber(state.nextPayoutAt) or 0
			hasState = true
			render()
		end
	end)

	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if hasState then
			render()
		end
	end)

	PrototypeGuiTheme.ApplyScreen(gui)
	intent:FireServer("Ready")
	render()
end

return EconomyController
]==========],"StarterPlayerScripts/Controllers/EconomyController.luau");
assert(game:GetService("StarterPlayer"):WaitForChild("StarterPlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("Controllers"):WaitForChild("MilitaryController").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

--[[
	Player-relevant Military / Armies V0.1 HUD.
	Recruit / RecruitMax / SetCommit / LockCommit intents only.
	Commitment field auto-submits parsed integers; Lock uses server-accepted intent.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local PrototypeGuiTheme = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Util"):WaitForChild("PrototypeGuiTheme"))
local MilitaryConfig = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Config"):WaitForChild("MilitaryConfig"))

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local MilitaryController = {}

local started = false
local COMMIT_DEBOUNCE_SEC = 0.08

local function decodeState(payload: any): any?
	if typeof(payload) == "string" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload)
		end)
		if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then
			return decoded
		end
		return nil
	elseif typeof(payload) == "table" then
		return payload
	end
	return nil
end

local function formatCountdown(endsAt: number): string
	local remaining = math.max(0, math.ceil(endsAt - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()))
	return string.format("%ds", remaining)
end

local function parseWholeNonNegative(text: string): number?
	if text == "" then
		return nil
	end
	local n = tonumber(text)
	if typeof(n) ~= "number" or n ~= n or n ~= math.floor(n) or n < 0 then
		return nil
	end
	return n
end

function MilitaryController.Start()
	if started or _G.__MilitaryV01Started == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__MilitaryV01Started = true

	local gui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 12)
	if not (gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		warn("[Military] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; HUD idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__MilitaryV01Started = nil
		return
	end

	local info = gui:WaitForChild("TerritoryInfo") :: Frame
	local selectedValue = info:WaitForChild("SelectedTerritoryId") :: StringValue
	local panel = gui:WaitForChild("MilitaryPanel") :: Frame
	local stats = panel:WaitForChild("Stats") :: TextLabel
	local recruitBox = panel:WaitForChild("RecruitBox") :: TextBox
	local costLabel = panel:WaitForChild("CostLabel") :: TextLabel
	local recruitButton = panel:WaitForChild("RecruitButton") :: TextButton
	local maxRecruitButton = panel:WaitForChild("MaxRecruitButton") :: TextButton
	local commitBody = panel:WaitForChild("CommitBody") :: TextLabel
	local commitBox = panel:WaitForChild("CommitBox") :: TextBox
	local lockButton = panel:WaitForChild("LockButton") :: TextButton
	local mapStage = gui:WaitForChild("MapStage") :: Frame
	local battleBanner = mapStage:WaitForChild("BattleBanner") :: TextLabel
	local resultToast = gui:WaitForChild("ResultToast") :: Frame
	local resultToastLabel = resultToast:WaitForChild("Label") :: TextLabel

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local intent = remotes:WaitForChild("MilitaryClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local push = remotes:WaitForChild("MilitaryServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent

	local total = 0
	local available = 0
	local committed = 0
	local troopsPerGold = 3
	local wars = {}
	local hasState = false
	local noticeToken = 0
	local lastSentByWar: { [string]: number } = {}
	local fieldStatus = ""
	local debounceToken = 0
	local boundSessionWarId: string? = nil
	local lastBattleByTerritory: { [string]: any } = {}
	local lastBattleTokenByTerritory: { [string]: number } = {}
	local COMMIT_BODY_IDLE = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 148)
	local COMMIT_BODY_SUMMARY = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 148)

	commitBox.PlaceholderText = "Troops to commit"
	lockButton.Text = "Ready"

	local function showNotice(message: string)
		noticeToken += 1
		local token = noticeToken
		resultToastLabel.Text = message
		resultToast.Visible = true
		task.delay(6, function()
			if token == noticeToken then
				resultToast.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end

	local function recruitAmount(): number?
		local n = tonumber(recruitBox.Text)
		if n == nil then
			return nil
		end
		return n
	end

	local function updateCost()
		local n = recruitAmount()
		if n == nil then
			costLabel.Text = "Gold cost: —"
			return
		end
		if n > 0 and n == math.floor(n) and n % troopsPerGold == 0 then
			costLabel.Text = string.format("Gold cost: %d", n / troopsPerGold)
		else
			costLabel.Text = "Gold cost: invalid"
		end
	end

	local function isLiveWar(war: any): boolean
		return war ~= nil and (war.phase == "commit" or war.phase == "present")
	end

	local function liveWars(): { any }
		local live = {}
		for _, war in wars do
			if isLiveWar(war) then
				table.insert(live, war)
			end
		end
		return live
	end

	local function warForTarget(territoryId: string): any?
		if territoryId == "" then
			return nil
		end
		for _, war in wars do
			if isLiveWar(war) and war.targetTerritoryId == territoryId then
				return war
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- Display/input context only. Selection never mutates server Military state.
	local function displayedWar(): any?
		local selected = selectedValue.Value
		local matched = warForTarget(selected)
		if matched then
			return matched
		end
		-- Empty selection + exactly one war: keep single-war HUD without a map click.
		if selected == "" then
			local live = liveWars()
			if #live == 1 then
				return live[1]
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function activeCommitWar(): any?
		return displayedWar()
	end

	local function resetCommitInput()
		debounceToken += 1
		fieldStatus = ""
		if commitBox.Text ~= "" then
			commitBox.Text = ""
		end
		commitBox.PlaceholderText = "Troops to commit"
	end

	local function lastBattleDuration(): number
		local override = panel:GetAttribute("DebugLastBattleSeconds")
		if typeof(override) == "number" and override == override and override > 0 then
			return override
		end
		local configured = tonumber(MilitaryConfig.LastBattleSummarySeconds)
		if configured and configured > 0 then
			return configured
		end
		return 20
	end

	local function lastBattleTerritoryId(result: any): string?
		if typeof(result.targetTerritoryId) == "string" and result.targetTerritoryId ~= "" then
			return result.targetTerritoryId
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function lastBattleForTerritory(territoryId: string): any?
		if territoryId == "" then
			return nil
		end
		return lastBattleByTerritory[territoryId]
	end

	local function soleCachedLastBattle(): any?
		local found: any? = nil
		local n = 0
		for _, result in lastBattleByTerritory do
			n += 1
			found = result
		end
		if n == 1 then
			return found
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function discardTerritoryLastBattle(territoryId: string)
		lastBattleTokenByTerritory[territoryId] = (lastBattleTokenByTerritory[territoryId] or 0) + 1
		lastBattleByTerritory[territoryId] = nil
	end

	local function formatLastBattle(result: any): string
		local place = result.targetDisplayName
		if typeof(place) ~= "string" or place == "" then
			place = result.targetTerritoryId
		end
		if typeof(place) ~= "string" or place == "" then
			place = "contested territory"
		end
		local outcome = if result.won == true then "VICTORY" else "DEFEAT"
		return table.concat({
			"Last Battle — " .. place,
			"",
			outcome,
			"",
			"Your committed: " .. tostring(tonumber(result.committed) or 0),
			"Enemy committed: " .. tostring(tonumber(result.enemyCommitted) or 0),
			"Your casualties: " .. tostring(tonumber(result.casualties) or 0),
			"Your survivors: " .. tostring(tonumber(result.survivors) or 0),
		}, "\n")
	end

	local function beginLastBattle(result: any)
		local territoryId = lastBattleTerritoryId(result)
		if territoryId == nil then
			return
		end
		lastBattleTokenByTerritory[territoryId] = (lastBattleTokenByTerritory[territoryId] or 0) + 1
		local token = lastBattleTokenByTerritory[territoryId]
		lastBattleByTerritory[territoryId] = result
		local displayed = displayedWar()
		if displayed == nil or displayed.targetTerritoryId == territoryId then
			resetCommitInput()
		end
		task.delay(lastBattleDuration(), function()
			if lastBattleTokenByTerritory[territoryId] == token then
				lastBattleByTerritory[territoryId] = nil
			end
		end)
	end

	local function syncCommitInputToSession()
		local session = displayedWar()
		local sessionId = if session and typeof(session.warId) == "string" then session.warId else nil
		if sessionId == nil then
			if boundSessionWarId ~= nil or commitBox.Text ~= "" then
				resetCommitInput()
			end
			boundSessionWarId = nil
			return
		end
		if boundSessionWarId == sessionId then
			return
		end
		debounceToken += 1
		fieldStatus = ""
		commitBox.PlaceholderText = "Troops to commit"
		boundSessionWarId = sessionId
		local intended = tonumber(session.intended)
		if typeof(intended) == "number" then
			lastSentByWar[sessionId] = intended
			local shown = tostring(intended)
			if commitBox.Text ~= shown then
				commitBox.Text = shown
			end
		elseif commitBox.Text ~= "" then
			commitBox.Text = ""
		end
	end

	local function maxCommitFor(war: any): number
		local intended = tonumber(war.intended) or 0
		return available + intended
	end

	local function trySendCommit(force: boolean): boolean
		local war = activeCommitWar()
		if not war or typeof(war.warId) ~= "string" then
			return false
		end
		if war.phase ~= "commit" or war.locked == true then
			return false
		end
		local parsed = parseWholeNonNegative(commitBox.Text)
		if parsed == nil then
			local text = commitBox.Text
			if text == "" then
				fieldStatus = ""
			else
				fieldStatus = "Invalid amount (not a whole number >= 0)"
			end
			return false
		end
		if parsed > maxCommitFor(war) then
			fieldStatus = "Invalid: over Available"
			if lastSentByWar[war.warId] == parsed and not force then
				return false
			end
			lastSentByWar[war.warId] = parsed
			intent:FireServer("SetCommit", war.warId, parsed)
			return false
		end
		if lastSentByWar[war.warId] == parsed and not force then
			fieldStatus = ""
			return true
		end
		fieldStatus = ""
		lastSentByWar[war.warId] = parsed
		intent:FireServer("SetCommit", war.warId, parsed)
		return true
	end

	local function scheduleCommitSend()
		debounceToken += 1
		local token = debounceToken
		task.delay(COMMIT_DEBOUNCE_SEC, function()
			if token == debounceToken then
				trySendCommit(false)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function render()
		if not hasState then
			stats.Text = "Total: —\nAvailable: —\nCommitted: —"
			commitBody.Text = "No active war commitment."
			commitBody.Size = COMMIT_BODY_IDLE
			commitBox.Visible = true
			lockButton.Visible = true
			battleBanner.Visible = false
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(recruitButton, true)
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(maxRecruitButton, true)
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(lockButton, false)
			lockButton.Active = false
			return
		end
		stats.Text = string.format("Total: %d\nAvailable: %d\nCommitted: %d", total, available, committed)
		updateCost()
		PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(recruitButton, true)
		PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(maxRecruitButton, true)
		recruitButton.Active = true
		maxRecruitButton.Active = true

		local function showLastBattleUi(result: any)
			syncCommitInputToSession()
			commitBox.Visible = false
			lockButton.Visible = false
			lockButton.Active = false
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(lockButton, false)
			battleBanner.Visible = false
			commitBody.Size = COMMIT_BODY_SUMMARY
			commitBody.Text = formatLastBattle(result)
		end

		local function showIdleCommitment(message: string)
			syncCommitInputToSession()
			commitBox.Visible = true
			lockButton.Visible = true
			lockButton.Active = false
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(lockButton, false)
			battleBanner.Visible = false
			commitBody.Size = COMMIT_BODY_IDLE
			commitBody.Text = message
		end

		local war = displayedWar()
		if war then
			local targetId = war.targetTerritoryId
			if typeof(targetId) == "string" then
				local cached = lastBattleForTerritory(targetId)
				if cached ~= nil and typeof(cached.warId) == "string" and cached.warId ~= war.warId then
					discardTerritoryLastBattle(targetId)
				end
			end
			commitBox.Visible = true
			lockButton.Visible = true
			commitBody.Size = COMMIT_BODY_IDLE
		else
			local selected = selectedValue.Value
			local selectedSummary = lastBattleForTerritory(selected)
			if selected ~= "" and selectedSummary ~= nil then
				showLastBattleUi(selectedSummary)
				return
			end
			if #liveWars() > 0 then
				showIdleCommitment("No commitment for this territory.\nSelect a contested territory.")
				return
			end
			if selected == "" then
				local only = soleCachedLastBattle()
				if only ~= nil then
					showLastBattleUi(only)
					return
				end
			end
			showIdleCommitment("No active war commitment.")
			return
		end

		local lines = {
			string.format("%s — %s", war.role or "?", war.targetDisplayName or war.targetTerritoryId),
			string.format("Available: %d", available),
		}
		if war.targetKind == "Unclaimed" then table.insert(lines, "Unclaimed militia: " .. tostring(war.neutralDefenders) .. " defenders") end
		if typeof(war.intended) == "number" then
			table.insert(lines, "Accepted commitment: " .. tostring(war.intended))
		else
			table.insert(lines, "Accepted commitment: none")
		end
		if fieldStatus ~= "" and war.phase == "commit" and war.locked ~= true then
			table.insert(lines, fieldStatus)
		end
		if war.phase == "commit" then
			if (tonumber(war.windowEndsAt) or 0) > workspace:GetServerTimeNow() then
				table.insert(lines, "Decision: " .. formatCountdown(war.windowEndsAt))
			else
				table.insert(lines, "Hard timeout: " .. formatCountdown(war.hardEndsAt))
			end
			if war.locked then
				table.insert(lines, war.opponentState or "Waiting for opponent")
			else
				table.insert(lines, war.opponentState or "Opponent preparing forces")
			end
		elseif war.phase == "present" then
			table.insert(lines, "Battle in progress")
			if typeof(war.attackerCommitted) == "number" and typeof(war.defenderCommitted) == "number" then
				table.insert(lines, string.format("Fielded: attacker %d / defender %d", war.attackerCommitted, war.defenderCommitted))
			end
			if typeof(war.presentationEndsAt) == "number" then
				table.insert(lines, "Resolving in " .. formatCountdown(war.presentationEndsAt))
			end
		end
		if war.result then
			table.insert(lines, war.result.title or "")
			table.insert(
				lines,
				string.format(
					"Committed %d / Casualties %d / Survivors %d",
					war.result.committed,
					war.result.casualties,
					war.result.survivors
				)
			)
		end
		commitBody.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")

		local canLock = war.phase == "commit" and war.locked ~= true and typeof(war.intended) == "number"
		lockButton.Active = canLock
		PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(lockButton, canLock)

		local showBattle = war.phase == "present"
		battleBanner.Visible = showBattle
		if showBattle then
			battleBanner.Text = "Battle in progress — " .. (war.targetDisplayName or "contested territory")
		end
	end

	recruitBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(updateCost)

	recruitButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local n = recruitAmount()
		intent:FireServer("Recruit", n)
	end)

	maxRecruitButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		intent:FireServer("RecruitMax")
	end)

	commitBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local war = activeCommitWar()
		if not war or war.phase ~= "commit" or war.locked == true then
			return
		end
		scheduleCommitSend()
	end)

	lockButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local war = activeCommitWar()
		if not war or typeof(war.warId) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		if not lockButton.Active then
			return
		end
		debounceToken += 1
		local parsed = parseWholeNonNegative(commitBox.Text)
		if parsed ~= nil and parsed <= maxCommitFor(war) then
			trySendCommit(true)
		end
		intent:FireServer("LockCommit", war.warId)
	end)

	selectedValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
		syncCommitInputToSession()
		render()
	end)

	push.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "BattleResult" then
			local result = decodeState(payload)
			if result and typeof(result.title) == "string" then
				showNotice(string.format(
					"%s  Committed %d / Casualties %d / Survivors %d",
					result.title,
					tonumber(result.committed) or 0,
					tonumber(result.casualties) or 0,
					tonumber(result.survivors) or 0
				))
				beginLastBattle(result)
			end
			return
		end
		if kind == "Notice" and typeof(payload) == "string" then
			showNotice(payload)
			if string.find(payload, "Not enough", 1, true) or string.find(payload, "Commitment", 1, true) then
				if string.find(payload, "Not enough", 1, true) then
					fieldStatus = "Invalid: over Available"
				elseif string.find(payload, "whole number", 1, true) or string.find(payload, "negative", 1, true) then
					fieldStatus = "Invalid amount"
				end
			end
			return
		end
		if kind ~= "State" then
			return
		end
		local state = decodeState(payload)
		if not state then
			return
		end
		total = tonumber(state.total) or 0
		available = tonumber(state.available) or 0
		committed = tonumber(state.committed) or 0
		troopsPerGold = tonumber(state.troopsPerGold) or 3
		wars = state.wars or {}
		hasState = true
		syncCommitInputToSession()
		for _, war in wars do
			if typeof(war.warId) == "string" and typeof(war.intended) == "number" then
				if lastSentByWar[war.warId] == nil then
					lastSentByWar[war.warId] = war.intended
				end
			end
		end
		render()
	end)

	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if hasState then
			render()
		end
	end)

	PrototypeGuiTheme.ApplyScreen(gui)
	intent:FireServer("Ready")
	render()
end

return MilitaryController

--[[
	Prototype GUI driver for Political Hierarchy V0.1.
	Displays replicated server state and sends untrusted intents only.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

local PrototypeGuiTheme = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Util"):WaitForChild("PrototypeGuiTheme"))

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local PoliticalHierarchyController = {}

local function bindGui(): ScreenGui?
	local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
	local chosen = playerGui:FindFirstChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui")
	if not (chosen and chosen:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		local waited = playerGui:WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 8)
		if waited and waited:IsA("ScreenGui") then
			chosen = waited
		end
	end
	if not (chosen and chosen:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		local template = StarterGui:FindFirstChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui")
		if template and template:IsA("ScreenGui") then
			chosen = template:Clone()
			chosen.Parent = playerGui
		end
	end
	if not (chosen and chosen:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		return nil
	end
	for _, child in playerGui:GetChildren() do
		if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and child.Name == "HierarchyPrototypeGui" and child ~= chosen then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
	return chosen
end

local started = false

function PoliticalHierarchyController.Start()
	if started or _G.__PoliticalHierarchyV01Started == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__PoliticalHierarchyV01Started = true

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local clientIntent = remotes:WaitForChild("HierarchyClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local serverPush = remotes:WaitForChild("HierarchyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent

	local gui = bindGui()
	if not gui then
		warn("[Hierarchy] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; controller idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__PoliticalHierarchyV01Started = nil
		return
	end
	gui.Enabled = true
	PrototypeGuiTheme.ApplyScreen(gui)
	print("[Hierarchy] controller started listening", player.Name)

	local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
	playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and child.Name == "HierarchyPrototypeGui" and child ~= gui then
			task.defer(function()
				if child.Parent then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	local main = gui:WaitForChild("Main")
	local selfInfo = main:WaitForChild("SelfInfo") :: TextLabel
	local treeView = main:WaitForChild("TreeScroll"):WaitForChild("TreeView") :: TextLabel
	local noticeLabel = main:WaitForChild("Notice") :: TextLabel
	noticeLabel.Visible = false
	local resultToast = gui:WaitForChild("ResultToast") :: Frame
	local resultToastLabel = resultToast:WaitForChild("Label") :: TextLabel
	local playerList = main:WaitForChild("PlayerList") :: ScrollingFrame
	local incomingList = main:FindFirstChild("IncomingList")
	local incomingHeader = main:FindFirstChild("IncomingHeader")
	if incomingHeader and incomingHeader:IsA("GuiObject") then
		incomingHeader.Visible = false
	end
	if incomingList and incomingList:IsA("GuiObject") then
		incomingList.Visible = false
	end
	local playerTemplate = gui:WaitForChild("Templates"):WaitForChild("PlayerRow")
	local offerCardTemplate = gui:WaitForChild("Templates"):WaitForChild("OfferCard")
	local offerOverlay = gui:WaitForChild("VassalageOfferOverlay") :: Frame
	offerOverlay.Visible = false
	local overlayExtra = offerOverlay:WaitForChild("ExtraList") :: Frame
	local overlayBody = offerOverlay:FindFirstChild("Body")
	local overlayButtons = offerOverlay:FindFirstChild("Buttons")
	if overlayBody and overlayBody:IsA("GuiObject") then
		overlayBody.Visible = false
	end
	if overlayButtons and overlayButtons:IsA("GuiObject") then
		overlayButtons.Visible = false
	end

	local myRulerId: string? = nil
	local receivedState = false
	local snapshot: any = { rulers = {}, requests = {} }
	local noticeToken = 0

	local function showNotice(message: string)
		noticeToken += 1
		local token = noticeToken
		resultToastLabel.Text = message
		resultToast.Visible = true
		noticeLabel.Text = message
		task.delay(6, function()
			if token == noticeToken then
				resultToast.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end

	local function rulerById(id: string): any?
		for _, ruler in snapshot.rulers do
			if ruler.rulerId == id then
				return ruler
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function myRuler(): any?
		if myRulerId then
			local found = rulerById(myRulerId)
			if found then
				return found
			end
		end
		for _, ruler in snapshot.rulers do
			if tonumber(ruler.userId) == player.UserId then
				myRulerId = ruler.rulerId
				return ruler
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function clearFrameChildren(container: Instance)
		for _, child in container:GetChildren() do
			if child:IsA("Frame") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	local function formatTree(): string
		local lines = {}
		local byId = {}
		for _, ruler in snapshot.rulers do
			byId[ruler.rulerId] = ruler
		end
		local function walk(id: string, linePrefix: string, childPrefix: string)
			local ruler = byId[id]
			if not ruler then
				return
			end
			table.insert(lines, linePrefix .. ruler.titleName .. " " .. ruler.displayName)
			for index, vassalId in ruler.vassalIds do
				local isLast = index == #ruler.vassalIds
				local branch = if isLast then "└── " else "├── "
				local nextChild = childPrefix .. (if isLast then "    " else "│   ")
				walk(vassalId, childPrefix .. branch, nextChild)
			end
		end
		for _, ruler in snapshot.rulers do
			if ruler.independent then
				walk(ruler.rulerId, "", "")
			end
		end
		if not receivedState then
			return "Waiting for server state..."
		end
		if #lines == 0 then
			return "(no rulers)"
		end
		return table.concat(lines, "\n")
	end

	local function refresh()
		local me = myRuler()
		if me then
			local liegeName = "none"
			if me.liegeId and me.liegeId ~= "" then
				local liege = rulerById(me.liegeId)
				liegeName = if liege then liege.displayName else me.liegeId
			end
			local vassalNames = {}
			for _, id in me.vassalIds do
				local v = rulerById(id)
				table.insert(vassalNames, if v then v.displayName else id)
			end
			selfInfo.Text = string.format(
				"Name: %s\nRank: %s\nStatus: %s\nDirect liege: %s\nDirect vassals: %s",
				me.displayName,
				me.titleName,
				if me.independent then "Independent" else "Subject",
				liegeName,
				if #vassalNames > 0 then table.concat(vassalNames, ", ") else "none"
			)
		else
			selfInfo.Text = if receivedState then "No ruler record yet." else "Waiting for server state..."
		end
		treeView.Text = formatTree()

		clearFrameChildren(playerList)
		local layoutOrder = 0
		local otherRows = 0
		for _, ruler in snapshot.rulers do
			local uid = tonumber(ruler.userId)
			local hasUser = ruler.hasUser == true or (uid ~= nil and uid ~= 0)
			if hasUser and uid ~= player.UserId then
				layoutOrder += 1
				otherRows += 1
				local row = playerTemplate:Clone()
				row.Name = "PlayerRow_" .. tostring(layoutOrder)
				row.Visible = true
				row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
				row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 34)
				row.Parent = playerList
				local nameLabel = row:WaitForChild("NameLabel") :: TextLabel
				nameLabel.Text = string.format("%s %s", ruler.titleName, ruler.displayName)
				nameLabel.Visible = true
				nameLabel.LayoutOrder = 1
				local offer = row:WaitForChild("OfferButton") :: TextButton
				offer.Visible = true
				offer.Active = true
				offer.LayoutOrder = 2
				offer.Text = "Offer Vassalage"
				PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(offer, true)
				offer.TextSize = 13
				offer.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					clientIntent:FireServer("Offer", uid)
				end)
			end
		end

		clearFrameChildren(overlayExtra)
		layoutOrder = 0
		local incomingCount = 0
		local meId = if me then me.rulerId else nil
		for _, request in snapshot.requests do
			if meId and request.toRulerId == meId then
				layoutOrder += 1
				incomingCount += 1
				local from = rulerById(request.fromRulerId)
				local fromName = if from then (from.titleName .. " " .. from.displayName) else request.fromRulerId
				local card = offerCardTemplate:Clone()
				card.Name = "OfferCard_" .. tostring(layoutOrder)
				card.Visible = true
				card.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
				card.Parent = overlayExtra
				local label = card:WaitForChild("Label") :: TextLabel
				label.Text = string.format("%s offers to become your vassal.", fromName)
				local accept = card:FindFirstChild("AcceptButton", true) :: TextButton
				local decline = card:FindFirstChild("DeclineButton", true) :: TextButton
				accept.Visible = true
				accept.Active = true
				decline.Visible = true
				decline.Active = true
				PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(accept, true)
				accept.TextSize = 14
				PrototypeGuiTheme.applyDangerButton(decline)
				decline.TextSize = 14
				accept.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					clientIntent:FireServer("Accept", request.requestId)
				end)
				decline.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					clientIntent:FireServer("Decline", request.requestId)
				end)
			end
		end
		offerOverlay.Visible = incomingCount > 0
		print(
			"[Hierarchy] render",
			player.Name,
			"rulers=",
			#(snapshot.rulers or {}),
			"otherRows=",
			otherRows,
			"incoming=",
			incomingCount
		)
	end

	serverPush.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "State" then
			local data = payload
			if typeof(payload) == "string" then
				local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload)
				end)
				if not ok or typeof(decoded) ~= "table" then
					warn("[Hierarchy] State JSON decode failed")
					return
				end
				data = decoded
			elseif typeof(payload) ~= "table" then
				warn("[Hierarchy] State payload not table/string", typeof(payload))
				return
			end
			receivedState = true
			snapshot = data
			if snapshot.rulers == nil then
				snapshot.rulers = {}
			end
			if snapshot.requests == nil then
				snapshot.requests = {}
			end
			print("[Hierarchy] State received", player.Name, "rulers=", #snapshot.rulers, "requests=", #snapshot.requests)
			refresh()
		elseif kind == "Notice" and typeof(payload) == "string" then
			showNotice(payload)
		end
	end)

	refresh()
	clientIntent:FireServer("Ready")
	print("[Hierarchy] remote Ready sent", player.Name)
end

return PoliticalHierarchyController

function Controller.Start()

			-- CoreGui may not have initialized yet; bounded startup retry.
			task.spawn(function()
				for _ = 1, 5 do
					task.wait(0.5)
					if pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList, false) end) then return end
				end

			end)

		if pending then return end
		pending = true
		task.defer(function() pending = false; refresh() end)
	end
	local function layout()

		compact = w < 720

			row.Relation.Visible = not compact
		end
	end
	refresh = function()
		layout()
		local connected = {}
		for _, current in Players:GetPlayers() do if not excluded[current.UserId] then table.insert(connected, current) end end
		local projected = View.Build(connected, player.UserId, states, sortMode)
		local alive, chosen = {}, nil
		for order, data in projected do
			local id = data.userId
			alive[id] = true

				row.Visible = true
				row.Parent = list

			row.LayoutOrder = order

			row:SetAttribute("PersonalHoldings", data.personal)
			row:SetAttribute("RealmHoldings", data.realm)
			row:SetAttribute("Relationship", data.relation)
			row.BackgroundColor3 = if selected == id then C.row elseif data.localPlayer then C.well else C.panel
			row.Accent.BackgroundColor3 = if data.localPlayer then C.accentGold elseif data.war then C.danger elseif data.allied then C.section else C.stroke
			row.Identity.Text = data.displayName .. (if data.localPlayer then "  ·  YOU" else "")
			row.Identity.TextColor3 = if data.localPlayer then C.accentGold else C.textPrimary
			row.Username.Text = "@" .. data.username .. (if compact then "  ·  " .. data.relation else "")
			row.Politics.Text = data.title .. "\n" .. data.status .. (if data.subjectCount > 0 then " · Liege" else "")
			row.Realm.Text = "Realm: " .. data.root .. (if data.status == "Subject" then "\nLiege: " .. data.liege else "")
			row.Holdings.Text = if compact then tostring(data.personal or "—") .. " / " .. tostring(data.realm or "—") .. " land" else tostring(data.personal or "—") .. " personal\n" .. tostring(data.realm or "—") .. " realm"
			row.Relation.Text = data.relation
			row.Relation.TextColor3 = if data.war then Color3.fromRGB(238, 135, 128) else C.section
			if selected == id then chosen = data end
		end
		for id, row in rows do if not alive[id] then row:Destroy(); rows[id] = nil end end
		if selected and not chosen then selected = nil end
		panel.Subtitle.Text = "Players — " .. #projected .. "   ·   Hold Tab to view"
		if chosen then
			detail.Text = chosen.title .. " " .. chosen.displayName .. "  ·  " .. chosen.status .. "  ·  " .. chosen.relation

				for _, other in panel.Sorts:GetChildren() do if other:IsA("TextButton") then other.BackgroundColor3 = if other == button then C.action else C.well end end
				schedule()
			end)
		end
	end
	panel.Close.Activated:Connect(function() Controller.SetOpen(false) end)
	gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(schedule)
	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(current) excluded[current.UserId] = nil; schedule() end)
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(current) excluded[current.UserId] = true; schedule() end)

			if kind ~= "State" then return end
			local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return if typeof(payload) == "string" then HttpService:JSONDecode(payload) else payload end)
			if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then states[system] = decoded; schedule() end
		end)
		local intent = remotes:WaitForChild(system .. "ClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
		intent:FireServer("Ready")
	end
	disableDefault()
	Controller.SetOpen(false)
	schedule()

	emptyRulers.Name = "EmptyRulers"
	emptyRulers.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	emptyRulers.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 48)
	emptyRulers.Text = "No other rulers in this match yet."
	emptyRulers.TextWrapped = true
	emptyRulers.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	emptyRulers.TextSize = 14
	emptyRulers.TextColor3 = C.textSecondary
	emptyRulers.Parent = gui.Main.PlayerList
	local function updateRulersEmpty()
		emptyRulers.Visible = gui.Main.PlayerList:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame") == nil
	end
	gui.Main.PlayerList.ChildAdded:Connect(updateRulersEmpty)
	gui.Main.PlayerList.ChildRemoved:Connect(updateRulersEmpty)
	updateRulersEmpty()
	local buttons = {}
	local layout: () -> ()
	local function position(item: GuiObject, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number)
		item.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
		item.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x,y)
		item.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w,h)
	end
	local function gotoPanel(name: string)
		selected = name
		gui:SetAttribute("SelectedSystem",name)
		for key, panelName in panels do
			(gui:FindFirstChild(panelName) :: GuiObject).Visible = key == name
		end
		for key, button in buttons do
			button.BackgroundColor3 = if key == name then C.action else C.well
			button.TextColor3 = if key == name then C.actionText else C.textSecondary
		end
		layout()
	end
	local function layer(item: Instance, z: number)
		if item:IsA("GuiObject") then item.ZIndex = z end
		for _, child in item:GetDescendants() do
			if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child.ZIndex = z + 1 end
		end
	end
	layout = function()
		local size = gui.AbsoluteSize
		local w,h = size.X,size.Y
		if w < 1 or h < 1 then return end
		local rail = if w < 760 then 80 else 100

		local open = selected ~= "Map"
		local left = rail + 20

		position(top,8,4,w-16,64)
		position(nav,8,76,rail,math.max(120,h-108))
		nav.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0,540)
		position(top.Gold,math.floor(w*0.42),8,math.floor(w*0.28)-20,48)
		position(top.Troops,math.floor(w*0.72),8,math.floor(w*0.28)-24,48)
		top.Gold.TextSize = if w < 760 then 12 else 14
		top.Troops.TextSize = if w < 760 then 12 else 14
		top.Ruler.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(120,math.floor(w*0.42)-32),22)
		position(map,left,76,mapWidth,math.max(160,h-108))
		position(hint,left,h-26,mapWidth,20)
		hint.Text = if gui:GetAttribute("MatchEnded") then "MATCH ENDED  ·  Inspect the map  ·  Players  ·  Match result" else (if top:GetAttribute("DominationRequired") then "Domination: " .. tostring(top:GetAttribute("DominationRequired")) .. " territories  ·  Drag to pan  ·  Wheel to zoom" else "Drag to pan  ·  Wheel to zoom  ·  Select a territory")
		for _, name in panels do
			local panel = gui:FindFirstChild(name) :: GuiObject
			position(panel,w-drawer-12,76,drawer,math.max(180,h-108))
			layer(panel,20)
		end
		local realm = gui.Main
		realm.TreeScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,110)
		realm.PlayerList.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,140)
		realm.SelfInfo.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,90)
		local dip = gui.DiplomacyPanel
		dip.Status.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,64)
		dip.List.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,math.max(130,h-238))
		local comms = gui.CommunicationsPanel
		local cw,ch = drawer,math.max(400,h-108)
		comms.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0,ch)
		position(comms.Header,12,10,cw-114,24)
		position(comms.MuteObserver,cw-88,10,76,24)

		position(comms.ContactList,12,164,cw-24,ch-260)

		position(comms.SendButton,cw-120,ch-86,50,32)
		position(comms.HelpButton,cw-64,ch-86,52,32)
		position(comms.DirectButton,12,ch-46,cw-134,32)
		position(comms.ObserverButton,cw-114,ch-46,102,32)
		local territory = gui.TerritoryInfo

		territory.WarStatus.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None

		local military = gui.MilitaryPanel
		military.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0,470)
		military.Stats.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,44)
		military.Stats.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,72)
		military.Stats.TextSize = 18
		military.RecruitBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,126)
		military.RecruitBox.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-18,0,32)
		military.RecruitButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,126)
		military.RecruitButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-66,0,32)
		military.MaxRecruitButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
		military.MaxRecruitButton.Position = UDim2.new(1,-60,0,126)
		military.MaxRecruitButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48,32)
		military.MaxRecruitButton.Text = "Max"
		military.CostLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,166)

		military.CommitBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,360)
		military.CommitBox.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,32)
		military.LockButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,402)
		military.LockButton.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,34)
		for i,name in {"VassalageOfferOverlay","DiplomacyOfferOverlay"} do
			local overlay = gui:FindFirstChild(name) :: GuiObject
			overlay.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
			overlay.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,h/2+(if i == 1 then -93 else 93))
			overlay.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(460,w-48),172)
			layer(overlay,100+i*4)
		end
		local toast = gui.ResultToast
		toast.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0)
		toast.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,76)
		toast.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(520,w-40),64)
		layer(toast,140)
		local inspect = gui:FindFirstChild("AdminInspectPanel")
		if inspect and inspect:IsA("GuiObject") then
			inspect.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
			inspect.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5)
			inspect.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(640,w-48),math.min(400,h-48))
			layer(inspect,160)
		end
	end
	for _, button in nav:GetChildren() do
		if button:IsA("TextButton") then
			local name = button.Name
			buttons[name] = button
			button.Activated:Connect(function()
				if name == "MatchResult" then return end
				if name == "Players" then Overview.SetOpen(true); return end
				if gui:GetAttribute("RealmOverviewOpen") then return end
				gotoPanel(if selected == name then "Map" else name)
			end)
			button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				if selected ~= name then button.BackgroundColor3=C.row end
			end)
			button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				button.BackgroundColor3 = if selected == name then C.action else C.well
			end)
		end
	end
	local function summaries()
		local economy = gui.EconomyPanel.Body.Text
		local gold = string.match(economy,"Gold: ([^\n]+)") or "—"
		local income = if gui:GetAttribute("MatchEnded") then "Income frozen · Match ended" else (string.match(economy,"Income: ([^\n]+)") or "Loading treasury")
		top.Gold.Text = "GOLD  "..gold.."\n"..income
		local stats = gui.MilitaryPanel.Stats.Text
		top.Troops.Text = "TROOPS  "..(string.match(stats,"Available: ([^\n]+)") or "—").." available\n"..(string.match(stats,"Committed: ([^\n]+)") or "0").." committed"
		local self = gui.Main.SelfInfo.Text
		top.Ruler.Text = if gui:GetAttribute("MatchEnded") and not string.match(self,"Name: ([^\n]+)") then "Spectator  ·  Match ended" else (string.match(self,"Rank: ([^\n]+)") or "Ruler").."  ·  "..(string.match(self,"Name: ([^\n]+)") or "Connecting")
		local war = gui.TerritoryInfo.WarStatus.Text
		buttons.Military.Text = if string.find(war,"No active wars",1,true) then "Military" else "Military  •"
	end
	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("MatchEnded"):Connect(summaries)
	for _, label in {gui.EconomyPanel.Body,gui.MilitaryPanel.Stats,gui.Main.SelfInfo,gui.TerritoryInfo.WarStatus} do
		label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(summaries)
	end
	local function style(inst: Instance)
		if inst:IsA("TextBox") then
			inst.BackgroundColor3=C.well; inst.TextColor3=C.textPrimary;inst.PlaceholderColor3=C.textMuted
			inst.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham;inst.TextSize=14;inst.BorderSizePixel=0
		elseif inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
			inst.ScrollBarThickness=5;inst.ScrollBarImageColor3=C.section
		end
		if inst:IsA("TextButton") and not inst:IsDescendantOf(nav) then
			inst.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if inst.Active then inst.BackgroundTransparency=0.12 end end)
			inst.MouseLeave:Connect(function() inst.BackgroundTransparency=0 end)
			inst.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() if inst.Active then inst.BackgroundTransparency=0.25 end end)
			inst.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() inst.BackgroundTransparency=0 end)
		end
	end
	for _, item in gui:GetDescendants() do style(item) end
	gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(style)
	gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(layout)
	gui.ChildAdded:Connect(function() task.defer(layout) end)
	local territoryValue = gui.TerritoryInfo.SelectedTerritoryId
	local previousCommitment: string? = nil
	gui.MilitaryPanel.CommitBody:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local text = gui.MilitaryPanel.CommitBody.Text
		local active = string.find(text, "Decision:", 1, true) or string.find(text, "Hard timeout:", 1, true)
		local key = if active then string.match(text, "^[^\n]+") else nil
		if key and key ~= previousCommitment then
			gui.MilitaryPanel.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
			gotoPanel("Military")
		end
		previousCommitment = key
	end)
	territoryValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
		if territoryValue.Value ~= "" and selected ~= "Military" then gotoPanel("Territory") end
	end)
	for _, name in {"VassalageOfferOverlay","DiplomacyOfferOverlay"} do
		local overlay=gui[name]
		overlay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
			nav.RequestNotice.Visible=gui.VassalageOfferOverlay.Visible or gui.DiplomacyOfferOverlay.Visible
		end)
	end
	local tabHeld = false
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if input.KeyCode ~= Bindings.HoldRealmOverview or processed or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
		tabHeld = true
		Overview.SetOpen(true)
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.KeyCode == Bindings.HoldRealmOverview and tabHeld then
			tabHeld = false
			Overview.SetOpen(false)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function() tabHeld = false; Overview.SetOpen(false) end)
	UserInputService.TextBoxFocused:Connect(function() if tabHeld then tabHeld = false; Overview.SetOpen(false) end end)
	summaries()
	gotoPanel("Map")
end
return Controller
]==========],"StarterPlayerScripts/Controllers/StrategyPresentationController.luau");
assert(game:GetService("StarterPlayer"):WaitForChild("StarterPlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("Controllers"):WaitForChild("TerritoryMapController").Source:gsub("\r\n","\n")==[==========[--!strict

--[[
	Prototype map renderer and input. Displays server ownership; never mutates it.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

local MapCatalog = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.MapCatalog)
local RealmColors = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Config.RealmColors)
local MapGeometry = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.MapGeometry)
local MapTriangles = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.MapTriangles)

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local TerritoryMapController = {}

local MIN_ZOOM = 0.7
local MAX_ZOOM = 2.8
local FOV = 25
local THICK = 6
local SELECT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 64)

local started = false

local function decodeState(payload: any): any?
	if typeof(payload) == "string" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload)
		end)
		if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then
			return decoded
		end
		return nil
	elseif typeof(payload) == "table" then
		return payload
	end
	return nil
end

local function scaleFromCentroid(polygon: { { x: number, y: number } }, towardCenter: number)
	local cx, cy = MapGeometry.Centroid(polygon)
	local out = {}
	for _, p in polygon do
		table.insert(out, {
			x = p.x + (cx - p.x) * towardCenter,
			y = p.y + (cy - p.y) * towardCenter,
		})
	end
	return out
end

function TerritoryMapController.Start()
	if started or _G.__TerritoryMapV01Started == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__TerritoryMapV01Started = true

	local gui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 12)
	if not (gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
		warn("[Territory] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; map idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__TerritoryMapV01Started = nil
		return
	end

	local mapStage = gui:WaitForChild("MapStage") :: Frame
	local viewport = mapStage:FindFirstChild("MapView")
	if not (viewport and viewport:IsA("ViewportFrame")) then
		if viewport then
			viewport:Destroy()
		end
		viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
		viewport.Name = "MapView"
		viewport.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 34, 42)
		viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
		viewport.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		viewport.ZIndex = 1
		viewport.Active = false
		viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210)
		viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
		viewport.Parent = mapStage
	end
	viewport.Active = false
	local info = gui:WaitForChild("TerritoryInfo") :: Frame
	local infoBody = info:WaitForChild("Body") :: TextLabel
	local selectedValue = info:FindFirstChild("SelectedTerritoryId")

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local territoryIntent = remotes:WaitForChild("TerritoryClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local territoryPush = remotes:WaitForChild("TerritoryServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	local hierarchyPush = remotes:WaitForChild("HierarchyServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent

	local mapDef = MapCatalog.GetDefault()
	local polygons = {}
	local byId = {}
	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		byId[t.id] = t
		table.insert(polygons, t.polygon)
	end
	local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = MapGeometry.MapBounds(polygons)
	local spanX = math.max(maxX - minX, 1)
	local spanY = math.max(maxY - minY, 1)
	local midX = (minX + maxX) * 0.5
	local midY = (minY + maxY) * 0.5

	local ownership: { [string]: string } = {}
	local colorIndex: { [string]: number } = {}
	local hierarchyById: { [string]: any } = {}
	local selectedId: string? = nil

	local zoom = 1
	local lookX = midX
	local lookZ = midY
	local dragging = false
	local dragMoved = false
	local dragStart: Vector2? = nil
	local lookStart = Vector2.zero

	local world = Instance.new("WorldModel")
	world.Parent = viewport
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "Territories"
	folder.Parent = world
	local fillParts: { [string]: { WedgePart } } = {}
	local rimParts: { [string]: { WedgePart } } = {}
	local selectParts: { [string]: { WedgePart } } = {}

	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		local selectFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
		selectFolder.Name = t.id .. "_select"
		selectFolder.Parent = folder
		local rim = Instance.new("Folder")
		rim.Name = t.id .. "_rim"
		rim.Parent = folder
		local fill = Instance.new("Folder")
		fill.Name = t.id .. "_fill"
		fill.Parent = folder
		-- Gold outline above fill/holder so selection does not replace political colors.
		selectParts[t.id] = MapTriangles.DrawRing(
			selectFolder,
			t.polygon,
			SELECT_COLOR,
			t.id .. "select",
			THICK,
			10,
			THICK * 0.75,
			true
		)
		for _, p in selectParts[t.id] do
			p.Transparency = 1
			p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			p.Color = SELECT_COLOR
		end
		rimParts[t.id] = MapTriangles.DrawQuad(rim, t.polygon, RealmColors.Unclaimed, t.id .. "rim", THICK, THICK * 0.2)
		fillParts[t.id] = MapTriangles.DrawQuad(
			fill,
			scaleFromCentroid(t.polygon, 0.18),
			RealmColors.Unclaimed,
			t.id .. "fill",
			THICK,
			THICK * 0.55
		)
	end

	local camera = Instance.new("Camera")
	camera.FieldOfView = FOV
	camera.Parent = viewport
	viewport.CurrentCamera = camera
	-- Noninteractive province names use the same projection as map picking.
	local provinceLabels = {}
	for _, t in mapDef.territories do
		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name = "ProvinceName_" .. t.id
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		label.Text = t.displayName
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
		label.TextSize = 12
		label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 34, 42)
		label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 231, 219)
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.65
		label.Active = false
		label.ZIndex = 2
		label.Parent = mapStage
		provinceLabels[t.id] = label
	end

	local function rulerOf(rulerId: string?): any?
		if not rulerId or rulerId == "" then
			return nil
		end
		return hierarchyById[rulerId]
	end

	local function ownerColor(ownerId: string): Color3
		local idx = colorIndex[ownerId]
		if typeof(idx) == "number" then
			return RealmColors.ByIndex(idx)
		end
		return RealmColors.ByIndex(1)
	end

	local function cameraHeight(): number
		local size = mapStage.AbsoluteSize
		local aspect = if size.Y > 0 then size.X / size.Y else 1
		local half = math.max(spanX / aspect, spanY) * 0.55 / zoom
		return half / math.tan(math.rad(FOV * 0.5))
	end

	local function applyCamera()
		local h = cameraHeight()
		local size = mapStage.AbsoluteSize
		local aspect = if size.Y > 0 then size.X / size.Y else 1
		local halfZ = h * math.tan(math.rad(FOV * 0.5))
		local halfX = halfZ * aspect
		local pad = 20
		local loX = minX - pad + halfX
		local hiX = maxX + pad - halfX
		if loX <= hiX then
			lookX = math.clamp(lookX, loX, hiX)
		else
			lookX = midX
		end
		local loZ = minY - pad + halfZ
		local hiZ = maxY + pad - halfZ
		if loZ <= hiZ then
			lookZ = math.clamp(lookZ, loZ, hiZ)
		else
			lookZ = midY
		end
		camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(lookX, h, lookZ), Vector3.new(lookX, 0, lookZ), Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
		for _, t in mapDef.territories do
			local cx, cy = MapGeometry.Centroid(t.polygon)
			local label = provinceLabels[t.id]
			local px = (0.5 + (cx - lookX) / (2 * halfX)) * size.X
			local py = (0.5 + (cy - lookZ) / (2 * halfZ)) * size.Y
			local x1, _, x2 = MapGeometry.PolygonBounds(t.polygon)
			local width = (x2 - x1) / (2 * halfX) * size.X * 0.85
			label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px, py)
			label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 24)
			label.TextSize = if width < 90 then 10 else 12
			label.Visible = width >= 54 and px > 0 and py > 0 and px < size.X and py < size.Y
		end
	end

	local function screenToMap(screen: Vector2): (number?, number?)
		local abs = viewport.AbsolutePosition
		local size = viewport.AbsoluteSize
		if size.X < 1 or size.Y < 1 then
			return nil, nil
		end
		local u = (screen.X - abs.X) / size.X
		local v = (screen.Y - abs.Y) / size.Y
		if u < 0 or v < 0 or u > 1 or v > 1 then
			return nil, nil
		end
		local h = cameraHeight()
		local aspect = size.X / size.Y
		local halfZ = h * math.tan(math.rad(FOV * 0.5))
		local halfX = halfZ * aspect
		local mx = lookX + (u - 0.5) * 2 * halfX
		local my = lookZ + (v - 0.5) * 2 * halfZ
		return mx, my
	end

	local function hitTerritory(mx: number, my: number): string?
		for _, t in mapDef.territories do
			if MapGeometry.PointInPolygon(mx, my, t.polygon) then
				return t.id
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function updateInfo()
		if not selectedId then
			infoBody.Text = "No territory selected.\nClick the map to inspect a province."
			return
		end
		local def = byId[selectedId]
		if not def then
			infoBody.Text = "Unknown territory."
			return
		end
		local ownerId = ownership[selectedId]
		if not ownerId or ownerId == "" then
			infoBody.Text = string.format(
				"%s\nID: %s\nOwner: Unclaimed\nRank: —\nStatus: —\nGreater realm: —",
				def.displayName,
				def.id
			)
			return
		end
		local owner = rulerOf(ownerId)
		local root = if owner then rulerOf(owner.rootRulerId) else nil
		infoBody.Text = string.format(
			"%s\nID: %s\nOwner: %s\nRank: %s\nStatus: %s\nGreater realm: %s",
			def.displayName,
			def.id,
			if owner then owner.displayName else ownerId,
			if owner then owner.titleName else "—",
			if owner then (if owner.independent then "Independent" else "Subject") else "—",
			if root then root.displayName else (if owner then owner.displayName else "—")
		)
	end

	local function colorize()
		for _, t in mapDef.territories do
			local ownerId = ownership[t.id]
			local fill = RealmColors.Unclaimed
			local rim = Color3.fromRGB(40, 44, 50)
			if ownerId and ownerId ~= "" then
				local owner = rulerOf(ownerId)
				local rootId = if owner and typeof(owner.rootRulerId) == "string" and owner.rootRulerId ~= "" then owner.rootRulerId else ownerId
				fill = ownerColor(rootId)
				rim = ownerColor(ownerId)
			end
			for _, p in fillParts[t.id] do
				p.Color = fill
				p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
			end
			for _, p in rimParts[t.id] do
				p.Color = rim
			end
			local selected = selectedId == t.id
			for _, p in selectParts[t.id] do
				p.Color = SELECT_COLOR
				p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
				p.Transparency = if selected then 0 else 1
			end
		end
		updateInfo()
		applyCamera()
		if selectedValue and selectedValue:IsA("StringValue") then
			selectedValue.Value = selectedId or ""
		end
	end

	local function applyTerritoryState(data: any)
		ownership = {}
		if typeof(data.territories) == "table" then
			for _, row in data.territories do
				if typeof(row.territoryId) == "string" then
					ownership[row.territoryId] = if row.hasOwner == true then (tostring(row.ownerRulerId)) else ""
				end
			end
		end
		colorIndex = {}
		if typeof(data.rulerColors) == "table" then
			for _, row in data.rulerColors do
				if typeof(row.rulerId) == "string" and typeof(row.colorIndex) == "number" then
					colorIndex[row.rulerId] = row.colorIndex
				end
			end
		end
		print("[Territory] State received map=", tostring(data.mapId))
		colorize()
	end

	local function applyHierarchyState(data: any)
		hierarchyById = {}
		if typeof(data.rulers) == "table" then
			for _, ruler in data.rulers do
				if typeof(ruler.rulerId) == "string" then
					hierarchyById[ruler.rulerId] = ruler
				end
			end
		end
		colorize()
	end

	territoryPush.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "State" then
			local data = decodeState(payload)
			if data then
				applyTerritoryState(data)
			else
				warn("[Territory] State decode failed")
			end
		end
	end)

	hierarchyPush.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "State" then
			local data = decodeState(payload)
			if data then
				applyHierarchyState(data)
			end
		end
	end)

	local function pointerScreen(): Vector2
		return UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() - GuiService:GetGuiInset()
	end

	local function mouseOverMap(screen: Vector2): boolean
		local abs = mapStage.AbsolutePosition
		local size = mapStage.AbsoluteSize
		return screen.X >= abs.X and screen.Y >= abs.Y and screen.X <= abs.X + size.X and screen.Y <= abs.Y + size.Y
	end

	-- Map is the lowest input layer. Higher project GUI at this point owns the pointer.
	-- ResultToast is informational and is not treated as a blocking layer.
	-- AdminInspectPanel, EconomyPanel, MilitaryPanel, and visible Kohl command/dashboard chrome block the map.
	local function isKohlBlockingGui(obj: GuiObject): boolean
		local screenGui = obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui")
		if not screenGui then
			return false
		end
		local guiName = screenGui.Name
		if
			guiName ~= "FluxUILayerTopbar"
			and guiName ~= "FluxUILayerTop"
			and guiName ~= "FluxUILayerDefault"
			and guiName ~= "FluxUILayerBottom"
		then
			return false
		end
		local current: Instance? = obj
		while current and current ~= screenGui do
			if current:IsA("GuiObject") and current.Visible then
				local n = current.Name
				if n == "CommandBar" or n == "Window" or n == "Dialog" or n == "Cmdbar2" or n == "KADashboardButton" or n == "KACommandsButton" then
					return true
				end
			end
			current = current.Parent
		end
		return false
	end
	local function isMapDescendant(obj: Instance): boolean
		local current: Instance? = obj
		while current and current ~= gui do
			if current == mapStage then
				return true
			end
			current = current.Parent
		end
		return false
	end

	local function blockingPanelOf(obj: Instance): GuiObject?
		local current: Instance? = obj
		while current and current ~= gui do
			if current:IsA("GuiObject") and current.Parent == gui then
				local name = current.Name
				if name == "VictoryOverlay" or name == "RealmOverviewOverlay" or name == "VassalageOfferOverlay" or name == "DiplomacyOfferOverlay" or name == "TitlePanel" or name == "Main" or name == "TerritoryInfo" or name == "DiplomacyPanel" or name == "AdminInspectPanel" or name == "EconomyPanel" or name == "MilitaryPanel" or name == "CommunicationsPanel" or name == "StrategyNavigation" or name == "StrategyTopbar" then
					return current
				end
			end
			current = current.Parent
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function pointerOnHigherGui(screen: Vector2): boolean
		local objects = player.PlayerGui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(screen.X, screen.Y)
		for _, obj in objects do
			if obj:IsA("GuiObject") and obj.Visible and not isMapDescendant(obj) then
				if isKohlBlockingGui(obj) then
					return true
				end
				local panel = blockingPanelOf(obj)
				if panel and panel.Visible then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local mapPress = false

	-- Press must start on the map surface (MapStage / MapView), not on higher panels.
	-- Drag uses inset-corrected cursor position so GuiObject vs UIS coordinates cannot cancel pan.
	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RealmOverviewOpen"):Connect(function()
		if gui:GetAttribute("RealmOverviewOpen") then mapPress = false; dragging = false; dragStart = nil end
	end)
	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("VictoryOverlayOpen"):Connect(function()
		if gui:GetAttribute("VictoryOverlayOpen") then mapPress = false; dragging = false; dragStart = nil end
	end)
	local function beginMapPress(input: InputObject)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end
		if gui:GetAttribute("RealmOverviewOpen") or pointerOnHigherGui(pointerScreen()) then
			return
		end
		mapPress = true
		dragging = true
		dragMoved = false
		dragStart = pointerScreen()
		lookStart = Vector2.new(lookX, lookZ)
	end

	mapStage.InputBegan:Connect(beginMapPress)
	viewport.InputBegan:Connect(beginMapPress)

	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input, _gameProcessed)
		if dragging and mapPress and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and dragStart then
			local now = pointerScreen()
			if (now - dragStart).Magnitude > 6 then
				dragMoved = true
			end
			if dragMoved then
				local size = viewport.AbsoluteSize
				if size.X < 1 or size.Y < 1 then
					return
				end
				local h = cameraHeight()
				local halfZ = h * math.tan(math.rad(FOV * 0.5))
				local halfX = halfZ * (size.X / size.Y)
				local dx = (now.X - dragStart.X) / size.X * 2 * halfX
				local dz = (now.Y - dragStart.Y) / size.Y * 2 * halfZ
				lookX = lookStart.X - dx
				lookZ = lookStart.Y - dz
				applyCamera()
			end
		elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
			-- Active MapStage marks wheel as gameProcessed. That is the map surface, not a higher panel.
			local pos = pointerScreen()
			if gui:GetAttribute("RealmOverviewOpen") or not mouseOverMap(pos) or pointerOnHigherGui(pos) then
				return
			end
			if input.Position.Z > 0 then
				zoom = math.clamp(zoom * 1.12, MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM)
			else
				zoom = math.clamp(zoom / 1.12, MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM)
			end
			applyCamera()
		end
	end)

	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end
		local wasMapPress = mapPress
		local wasDrag = dragMoved
		mapPress = false
		dragging = false
		dragMoved = false
		if not wasMapPress or wasDrag then
			return
		end
		local pos = pointerScreen()
		if pointerOnHigherGui(pos) then
			return
		end
		local mx, my = screenToMap(pos)
		if not mx or not my then
			return
		end
		selectedId = hitTerritory(mx, my)
		colorize()
	end)

	mapStage:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
		applyCamera()
	end)

	applyCamera()
	territoryIntent:FireServer("Ready")
	print("[Territory] controller started; Ready sent")
	colorize()
end

return TerritoryMapController

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

local function decodeState(payload: any): any?
	if typeof(payload) == "string" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload)
		end)
		if ok and typeof(decoded) == "table" then
			return decoded
		end
		return nil
	elseif typeof(payload) == "table" then
		return payload
	end
	return nil

function TitleProgressionController.Start()
	if started or _G.__TitleProgressionV01Started == true then
		return
	end
	started = true
	_G.__TitleProgressionV01Started = true

	local gui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild("HierarchyPrototypeGui", 12)

		warn("[Title] HierarchyPrototypeGui missing; title panel idle.")
		started = false
		_G.__TitleProgressionV01Started = nil
		return
	end

	local panel = gui:WaitForChild("TitlePanel") :: Frame
	local body = panel:WaitForChild("Body") :: TextLabel
	local establish = panel:WaitForChild("EstablishButton") :: TextButton
	local resultToast = gui:WaitForChild("ResultToast") :: Frame

	local noticeToken = 0

	local function showNotice(message: string)
		noticeToken += 1
		local token = noticeToken
		resultToastLabel.Text = message
		resultToast.Visible = true
		task.delay(6, function()
			if token == noticeToken then
				resultToast.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end

	local function applyRow(row: any?)
		myRow = row
		-- Keep the Establish slot reserved so TitlePanel geometry does not reflow.
		establish.Visible = true
		if not row then
			body.Text = "Current title: —\nNext title: —\nRealm control: —\nTest Prestige: —\nStatus: Waiting for server state..."
			establish.Text = "Establish"
			establish.Active = false
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(establish, false)
			return
		end
		local nextLabel = if row.hasNext == true and row.nextTitleName ~= "" then row.nextTitleName else "—"
		local realm = if row.hasNext == true
			then string.format("%d / %d", row.realmControl, row.realmRequired)
			else string.format("%d", row.realmControl)
		local prestige = if row.hasNext == true
			then string.format("%d / %d", row.prestige, row.prestigeRequired)
			else string.format("%d", row.prestige)
		body.Text = string.format(
			"Current title: %s\nNext title: %s\nRealm control: %s\nTest Prestige: %s\nStatus: %s",
			row.currentTitleName,
			nextLabel,
			realm,
			prestige,
			row.statusText
		)
		if row.hasNext == true then
			establish.Text = row.establishLabel
			local can = row.eligible == true
			establish.Active = can
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(establish, can)
		else
			establish.Text = "No further title"
			establish.Active = false
			PrototypeGuiTheme.applyPrimaryButton(establish, false)
		end
	end

	titlePush.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind: any, payload: any)
		if kind == "Notice" and typeof(payload) == "string" then
			showNotice(payload)
			return
		end
		if kind ~= "State" then
			return
		end
		local data = decodeState(payload)
		if not data or typeof(data.rows) ~= "table" then
			warn("[Title] State decode failed")
			return
		end
		local mine = nil
		for _, row in data.rows do
			if tonumber(row.userId) == player.UserId then
				mine = row
				break
			end
		end
		applyRow(mine)
	end)

	establish.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not myRow or myRow.eligible ~= true then
			return
		end
		titleIntent:FireServer("Establish")
	end)

	applyRow(nil)
	titleIntent:FireServer("Ready")
	print("[Title] controller started; Ready sent")
end

return TitleProgressionController

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Controller = {}

function Controller.Start()

	local player = Players.LocalPlayer

	local overlay = gui:WaitForChild("VictoryOverlay") :: Frame

		end

		local function disable()
			if gui:GetAttribute("MatchEnded") == true and item.Active then
				item.Active = false
				Theme.applyPrimaryButton(item, false)
			end
		end
		item:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Active"):Connect(disable)
		gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("MatchEnded"):Connect(disable)

	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
	local push = remotes:WaitForChild("VictoryServerEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	push.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind, payload)
		if kind ~= "State" then return end
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(payload) end)
		if not ok or typeof(decoded) ~= "table" then return end
		state = decoded
		local ended = state.ended == true
		gui:SetAttribute("MatchEnded", ended)
		gui.MapHint.Text = if ended then "MATCH ENDED  ·  Inspect the map  ·  Players  ·  Match result" else string.format("DOMINATION: %d / %d territories required  ·  Expand your greater realm", state.required, state.total)
		gui.StrategyNavigation.MatchResult.Visible = ended
		gui.StrategyTopbar:SetAttribute("DominationRequired", state.required)
		if not ended then return end
		local member = false
		for _, ruler in state.members or {} do if ruler.userId == player.UserId then member = true end end
		panel.Heading.Text = if state.winnerUserId == player.UserId then "VICTORY" elseif member then "REALM VICTORY" else "DEFEAT"
		panel.Winner.Text = state.winnerTitle .. " " .. state.winnerName
		panel.Body.Text = state.realmName .. "\n\n" .. string.format("%d / %d territories  ·  %.1f%% controlled", state.controlled, state.total, state.controlPercent * 100)

			.. "\n\nThe match has ended. Competitive actions are frozen.\nInspect the map, view Players or continue communicating."
		if not shown then overlay.Visible = true; shown = true end
		layout()
	end)
	local intent = remotes:WaitForChild("VictoryClientEvent") :: RemoteEvent
	intent:FireServer("Ready")
	layout()
end

return Controller
